DHS recognizes that these reforms will apply equally to aliens with meritorious and meritless asylum claims and that either population may experience some degree of economic hardship as a result of heightened requirements for an EAD, the extended waiting period, and the pauses in USCIS' acceptance of EAD applications from asylum applicants. DHS also recognizes that some aliens whose asylum applications would have been found meritorious— i.e., those who would be able to show a well-founded fear of persecution in their country of nationality (or last habitual residence) on account of a protected ground—may abandon their applications or decide not to file applications and forego the protection that asylum would provide because they would not be able to support themselves while their asylum application is adjudicated. DHS recognizes that extending the processing time for employment authorization may also factor into a potentially meritorious applicant's decision-making process before applying for asylum. Due to this rule's proposed increased waiting periods before an alien may receive employment authorization, there may be aliens with potentially meritorious asylum claims who instead return to a country where they may fear harm. DHS has seriously considered the potential harm to this population and has determined that the benefits of this rule outweigh these concerns: increasing program integrity, focusing USCIS resources on the underlying asylum backlog, ensuring aggravated felons and criminal aliens are not granted work authorization, biometrically verifying the identity of all (c)(8) EAD applicants and identifying any criminal history, if applicable, and disincentivizing asylum as a means to file a frivolous, fraudulent, or meritless application solely to obtain work authorization. Objectively speaking, the asylum system is overwhelmed and in need of additional reforms. The backlog of asylum cases weakens the integrity of the system, allowing thousands of non-meritorious cases to languish and obstructing the agency from addressing potential public safety and national security concerns until years down the road when the cases are finally adjudicated. The security of the United States and the integrity of our immigration processes outweighs the potential harm to a subset of the asylum applicant population. DHS has also considered potential hardship caused by a lengthier wait before filing an application for employment authorization or receiving employment authorization, which may lead some aliens to attempt to work without authorization. In order to minimize unauthorized employment, DHS has instituted certain compliance measures through the Immigration Reform and Control Act (IRCA), which requires employers to verify the identity and employment eligibility of their employees and sets forth criminal and civil sanctions for employment-related violations. See Public Law 99-603, 100 Stat. 3445 (1986). Additionally, section 274A(b) of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1324a(b), requires employers to verify the identity and employment eligibility of all aliens hired in the United States. The Employment Eligibility Verification form (Form I-9) is used by employers to document this verification. Employers who fail to properly complete Forms I-9 are subject to civil money penalties for paperwork violations.[161] This process serves to protect the public and aliens who may attempt to work without authorization, which makes those aliens vulnerable to exploitation by their employers. Aliens who still choose to engage in unauthorized employment should be aware that this may render them removable and ineligible for future benefits such as adjustment of status.[162] Finally, DHS acknowledges there may be unknown impacts to the above populations, but DHS's responsibility to safeguarding national security and public safety takes precedence and justifies the approach proposed here.
国土安全部（DHS）的最终目标是加强庇护程序的福利诚信度，并帮助确保该制度不被滥用。国土安全部已确定，庇护申请人获得工作许可的现行模式已不再可行，且与庇护制度的初衷不符。该制度的初衷始终是，一旦提交庇护申请，应及时作出决定，从而在外国人获得该项福利之前无需工作许可文件。国土安全部已确定，在庇护申请人申请并获得就业许可文件（EAD）之前，要求其满足额外的时间和安全要求是合理的。保护国家安全、公共安全以及维护美国庇护和移民制度完整性的紧迫性，超过了有理由的庇护申请人群体在获得就业许可文件之前可能因额外等待期而承受的困难。
DHS's ultimate goal is to strengthen the benefit integrity of the asylum process and help ensure that the system is not being exploited. DHS has determined that the current model for obtaining employment authorization as an asylum applicant is no longer practicable, but also inconsistent with the original intent of the asylum system. The intent has always been that once an asylum claim is filed, a decision is made in a timely manner so that there is no need for an employment authorization document until the alien has received the benefit. DHS has determined it is reasonable to require additional time and security requirements on asylum applicants before they may apply for and receive an EAD. The urgency to protect national security, public safety, and maintain the integrity of the U.S. asylum and immigration system outweighs the hardship that may be imposed by an additional waiting period the meritorious asylum applicant population would experience prior to receiving an EAD.
一、已考虑的其他监管替代方案
1. Other Regulatory Alternatives Considered
国土安全部在最终决定本规则所提出的变更之前，考虑了若干替代方案，并且最近还实施了影响庇护申请及基于待决庇护申请的工作许可申请的新申请费。
DHS considered several alternatives before deciding on the changes ultimately proposed in this rule and also recently implemented new filing fees that impact both asylum applications and pending asylum application-based applications for employment authorization.
2025年7月22日，美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）发布了《H.R.-1联邦公报通知》，向公众通报根据《One Big Beautiful Bill Act》（OBBBA）设立的多项移民相关表格新收费标准。[163] USCIS最近实施了法定强制性的申请费，包括庇护申请不可豁免的100美元申请费，以及申请人庇护申请待决期间每年100美元的年费，还有首次(c)(8)工作许可申请不可豁免的550美元申请费。[164] 根据法律规定，庇护申请费的50%计入国土安全部账户。年费收入不计入USCIS账户，(c)(8)工作许可申请费的25%计入USCIS账户。
On July 22, 2025, USCIS published the H.R.-1 Federal Register Notice to inform the public of a new series of fees for various immigration-related forms established in the OBBBA.[163] USCIS recently implemented statutorily-mandated filing fees, including a $100 non-waivable filing fee for the asylum application and $100 annual fee for every year the applicant's asylum application is pending, as well as a $550 non-waivable filing fee for the initial (c)(8) employment authorization application.[164] Per statute, 50 percent of the asylum application fee is credited to DHS. None of the annual fee revenue is credited to USCIS and 25-percent of the (c)(8) employment authorization application fees are credited to USCIS.
从历史上看，仅凭费用变化并未导致福利申请数量的显著变化，尤其是在没有其他替代方案的情况下。[165] 因此，国土安全部认为，仅凭《H.R.-1》法案中新设的庇护申请费不足以遏制本规则所指出的不可持续数量的无理庇护申请，尽管国土安全部认为，这些费用有可能增强本拟议规则在阻止无理、欺诈或其他无理庇护申请方面的效果。此外，如本拟议规则第III.B节和第III.C节所述，并经近期USCIS规则制定文件89 FR 101210（2024年12月13日）所讨论， （印刷页8645）USCIS为加快处理庇护申请和(c)(8)工作许可申请而投入额外资源的努力，始终未能跟上申请量的快速增长。国土安全部认为，这是因为长期存在的庇护积案导致工作许可期限延长，从而激励了更多庇护申请的提出。[166]
Historically, fee changes alone have not caused significant changes in benefits requests, particularly when there are no alternatives.[165] Therefore, DHS does not think that the new asylum application fees from H.R.-1 alone are sufficient to dissuade the unsustainable volumes of meritless asylum claims identified in this rule, although DHS believes that it is possible that the fees may enhance the effects of this proposed rule to deter frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications. Furthermore, as described in sections III.B and III.C of this proposed rule, and discussed by recent USCIS rulemakings 89 FR 101210 (Dec. 13, 2024), USCIS efforts to apply ( printed page 8645) additional resources toward faster processing of asylum and (c)(8) employment authorization applications have consistently failed to match rapid growth in volumes. DHS argues this is because the employment authorization for longer durations caused by persistent asylum backlogs have incentivized more asylum claims.[166]
国土安全部（DHS）考虑并评估的另一种备选方案是重新发布2020年取消30天工卡（EAD）处理时限规则（“时限废止规则”），但采用更新的申请数据、更新近的经济分析以及对所提议变更的额外说理。国土安全部认识到，根据《移民与国籍法》第274A(h)(3)(B)条（8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B)）、第208(d)(1)条和第(d)(5)(B)条（8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1)和(d)(5)(B)）以及第208(d)(2)条（8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)），部长拥有作出此类变更的权限。然而，国土安全部注意到CASA de Maryland, Inc. 诉 Wolf 案的判决，该判决认定取消30天庇护工卡时限（“时限废止规则”）因多种不同原因而属于武断和反复无常的行为。该法院认定，美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）取消30天处理时限的理由与记录中的证据相悖，且USCIS对公众意见的回应流于结论性，表明该机构未考虑重要的政策替代方案。[167] 具体而言，法院并不信服USCIS曾考虑过延长处理时限而非彻底取消该时限这一做法。[168] 尽管国土安全部仍然认为(c)(8)类工卡不应设处理时限，正如目前其他任何工卡类别也都没有处理时限一样，但国土安全部对于即便采用更新的申请数据、更新近的经济分析、对替代方案的进一步考量以及额外的说理，再次提议彻底取消(c)(8)类工卡处理时限是否能够成功，仍存在不确定性。法规修订需要投入大量工作，国土安全部不愿再冒险耗费数年之功，最终却仍遭受不利的法院裁决，并最终仍须依据当前存在缺陷的法规权限和时限审理待决的庇护申请及相关的工作许可申请。
One alternative DHS considered and evaluated was the possibility of re-publishing the elimination of the 30-day EAD processing timeframe rule (“Timeline Repeal Rule”) from 2020, but with updated filing data, more recent economic analysis, and additional justification for the proposed changes. DHS recognizes that any such changes are within the Secretary's authority under INA 274A(h)(3)(B) (8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B)), INA 208(d)(1) and (d)(5)(B) (8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1) and (d)(5)(B)), and INA 208(d)(2) (8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)). However, DHS is mindful of the CASA de Maryland, Inc. v. Wolf holding that determined the elimination of the 30-day Asylum EAD clock (“Timeline Repeal Rule”) was arbitrary and capricious for multiple different reasons. That court found that USCIS' rationale for elimination of the 30-day processing timeframe belied the evidence in the record and USCIS' responses to public comments were conclusory and reflected that the agency did not consider important policy alternatives.[167] Specifically, the court was not convinced that USCIS considered imposing a longer processing timeframe instead of removing the timeframe altogether.[168] Despite the fact that DHS still believes there should be no processing timeframe on (c)(8) EADs—just as there are currently no processing timeframes on any other EAD category—DHS was uncertain if a second proposed outright elimination of the (c)(8) EAD processing timeframe would be successful even with updated filing data, more recent economic analysis, additional consideration of alternatives, and additional justifications. A significant amount of work goes into regulatory changes, and DHS would rather not risk another years long effort merely to be subject to adverse court action and, in the end, still be required to adjudicate pending asylum applications and associated employment authorization applications under the current, and flawed, regulatory authorities and timeframes.
国土安全部考虑并评估的第二种备选方案是将基于待决庇护申请提出工作许可申请的等待期从目前的150天延长至显著更长的期限，接近四年或五年。延长该等待期完全在部长依据《移民与国籍法》第274A(h)(3)(B)条（8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B)）、第208(d)(1)条和第(d)(5)(B)条（8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1)和(d)(5)(B)）以及第208(d)(2)条（8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)）所享有的权限范围之内，这些条款明确承认部长拥有向外国人授予工作许可的自由裁量权，拥有就庇护申请的程序和条件制定法规的权限，以及在自庇护申请提交之日起满180天后，对申请庇护的外国人授予工作许可的裁量权。此类延长的益处在于，它基本上可以消除上文所讨论的所有筛查与审查方面的障碍（例如，申请人支持中心（ASC）预约延误、向移民及海关执法局（ICE）转介的60天暂停期等），同时也将消除外国人为获得工作许可而提交无实质内容、欺诈性或其他无根据的庇护申请的动机。在这样的提案下，实际上很少有外国人会真正等待五年才获得其首次工作许可，因为庇护案件目前按照后进先出（LIFO）方式处理，因此绝大多数近期庇护申请人将在不到五年的时间内获得最终裁决。即便不采取所提议的法规变更，国土安全部也需要改善其运营以及筛查与审查工作，在2022财年、2023财年和2024财年，获得最终决定（批准、行政结案、拒绝/转介）的庇护申请的平均处理时间分别为35.5个月、25.0个月和22.8个月。[169] 尽管这些处理时间远远超过《移民与国籍法》第208(d)(5)(A)(iii)条规定的180天目标，但其正朝着正确方向发展，且短于所考虑的四年或五年备选方案。
A second alternative DHS considered and evaluated was extending the waiting period for filing an application for employment authorization based on a pending asylum application from the current 150 days to a significantly longer period, something closer to four or five years. Extending this waiting period would be well within the Secretary's authority under INA 274A(h)(3)(B) (8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B)), INA 208(d)(1) and (d)(5)(B) (8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1) and (d)(5)(B)), and INA 208(d)(2) (8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)), which clearly recognize the discretionary authority to extend employment authorization to aliens, the authority to establish regulations concerning the procedures and conditions on asylum applications, and the discretion to grant employment authorization to aliens applying for asylum if 180 days have passed since filing the application for asylum. The benefits of such an extension are that it would essentially remove all screening and vetting roadblocks discussed above ( e.g., ASC appointment delays, 60 day-pause for referrals to ICE, etc.) and it would also remove any incentive for aliens to file frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications in order to receive employment authorization. Under such a proposal, very few aliens would actually wait five years for their initial employment authorization because asylum cases are currently worked under LIFO processing, so the overwhelming majority of recent asylum applicants would receive a final adjudication in less than five years. Even without the proposed regulatory changes DHS needs to improve operations as well as screening and vetting, in FY2022, FY2023, and FY2024, the average processing time for asylum applications that received a final decision (approval, administrative closure, denial/referral) was 35.5 months, 25.0 months, and 22.8 months, respectively.[169] While the processing times far exceed the 180-day target provided in INA 208(d)(5)(A)(iii), they are trending in the right direction and are less than the four or five year alternative proposal considered.
国土安全部(DHS)最终决定不将150天工卡时钟延长至此幅度,原因有以下几项。虽然四到五年的等待期将对无根据、欺诈性或无实质理由的申请构成强有力的抑制因素,但这很可能招致移民权益倡导者以及可能将这一固定且漫长的等待期变化视为过度严苛的庇护申请人的强烈反对。虽然拟议的暂停与重启方法也可能导致长达数年的等待,但与本第二种备选方案所提议的变化不同,该暂停是可以解除的。最终,国土安全部认定,尽管这将对无实质理由的申请构成非常强有力的抑制因素,但会有大量强烈反对此类变更的公众意见,且此类期限延长的理据可能得不到数据的支持。
DHS ultimately decided not to extend the 150-day EAD clock this far for several reasons. While a four to five year waiting period would be a strong disincentive for frivolous, fraudulent, or meritless applications, this would likely lead to strong opposition from immigration advocates and asylum applicants who may view this fixed and lengthy change in the waiting period as unduly harsh. While the proposed pause and restart method will likely lead to a years-long wait as well, that pause can be lifted, unlike the change proposed in this second alternative. In the end, DHS determined that while a very strong disincentive for meritless filings, there would be numerous and strong public comments that did not support such a change, and the justification for such an extension of that duration may not be supported by data.
国土安全部考虑的另一项备选方案是彻底终止对待决庇护申请人的就业授权,换言之,取消(c)(8)类工卡类别。取消(c)(8)类工卡类别完全属于《移民与国籍法》(INA)第274A(h)(3)(B)条(8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B))、第208(d)(1)条及第(d)(5)(B)条(8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1)及(d)(5)(B))以及第208(d)(2)条(8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2))所赋予部长的职权范围之内,这些条款明确承认部长拥有向外国人延长就业授权的自由裁量权、制定有关庇护申请程序与条件的法规的权力,以及在自提交庇护申请之日起已满180天的情况下,对申请庇护的外国人给予就业授权的自由裁量权。根据法律规定,庇护申请待决的外国人并无获得就业授权的当然权利,但国会授权部长在庇护申请待决审理期间,通过制定法规自由裁量地授权此类外国人就业。参见INA第208(d)(2)条,8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)。此备选方案将无需进行筛查和审查,因为外国人将不再提交就业授权申请。此备选方案实际上将成为对无根据、欺诈性或无实质理由的庇护申请最强有力的可能抑制因素,其方式不是增加延误,而是彻底取消临时就业授权作为提交庇护申请的激励因素。此方案将消除在积压案件中长期保有无实质理由但待决的庇护申请所带来的任何益处。(原文第8646页)在此类提案下,由于没有(c)(8)类工卡可供申请,庇护申请待决的外国人在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)批准其基础庇护申请之前,将不具备就业授权资格。
Another alternative considered by DHS was ending employment authorization for pending asylum applicants altogether, in other words, terminating the (c)(8) EAD category. Eliminating the (c)(8) EAD category would be well within the Secretary's authority under INA 274A(h)(3)(B) (8 U.S.C. 1324a(h)(3)(B)), INA 208(d)(1) and (d)(5)(B) (8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1) and (d)(5)(B)), and INA 208(d)(2) (8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2)), which clearly recognize the discretionary authority to extend employment authorization to aliens, the authority to establish regulations concerning the procedures and conditions on asylum applications, and the discretion to grant employment authorization to aliens applying for asylum if 180 days have passed since filing the application for asylum. An alien with a pending asylum application is not entitled to employment authorization by statute, but Congress granted the Secretary discretion to authorize employment, through regulations, for these aliens while the asylum application is pending adjudication. See INA 208(d)(2), 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2). This alternative would obviate the need to screen and vet because there would be no application for employment authorization submitted by the alien. This alternative would really be the strongest disincentive possible for filing frivolous, fraudulent, or meritless asylum filings, not by adding a delay but by completely eliminating temporary employment authorization as an incentive for filing an asylum application. This option would eliminate any benefit to having a pending, but meritless asylum application in the backlog for years. ( printed page 8646) Under such a proposal, with no (c)(8) EAD to apply for, aliens with pending asylum applications would not be employment authorized until USCIS approved the underlying asylum application.
国土安全部最终决定目前不采取此类备选方案。首先,国土安全部已经确定,主要问题在于处理积压案件数量的能力。其次,由于待决庇护申请人的就业授权已存在数十年之久,早于移民归化局(INS)1994年庇护改革规则制定之前即已存在,目前尚不清楚是否存在支持此类变更的数据。此外,国土安全部担忧预期的公众意见将不支持此类变更。另外,国土安全部认为,本规则中拟议的条款,即在庇护案件平均处理时间连续90天超过180天时暂停受理初次(c)(8)类工卡申请,将就业授权与庇护处理时间相挂钩,能够以较为缓和的方式实现与彻底取消(c)(8)类工卡类别相同的积极效果。
DHS ultimately decided not to pursue such an alternative at this time. First, DHS has already established that the primary problem is the processing of the volume of cases in the backlog. Second, because employment authorization for pending asylum applicants has been available for decades, since prior to the INS's 1994 asylum reform rulemakings, it is not clear at this time whether data exists to support such a change. Moreover, DHS was concerned with the anticipated public comments that did not support such a change. Additionally, DHS believes that the proposed provision of this rule tethering employment authorization to asylum processing times by pausing the acceptance of initial (c)(8) EADs if average asylum processing rises above 180 days for 90 consecutive days would achieve the same positive impact that terminating the (c)(8) EAD category altogether would achieve but through less severe means.
倘若本规则被证明无效或被禁令阻止实施,鉴于这些备选方案所代表的持续激励非法进入美国以及滥用庇护制度、损害具有实质理由的庇护寻求者利益的情况,国土安全部很可能会针对未来的庇护申请及其相关的就业授权申请,重新评估上述一项或多项备选方案。
Should this rule prove ineffective or be enjoined, DHS will likely re-evaluate one or more of these alternative options for future asylum applications and their associated employment authorization applications given the ongoing incentive they represent for illegal entry to the United States and abuse of the asylum system to the detriment of meritorious asylum seekers.
D. 背景
D. Background
1. 庇护资格
1. Eligibility for Asylum
庇护是一项酌情给予的待遇,如果外国人证实其因种族、宗教、国籍、属于特定社会群体或政治见解而遭受过迫害或存在对未来迫害的合理恐惧等情形,部长或司法部长可酌情准予庇护。《移民与国籍法》(INA)第208(b)(1)条,8 U.S.C. 1158(b)(1)(规定司法部长和部长“可以”向难民准予庇护);《移民与国籍法》第101(a)(42)(A)条,8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(42)(A)(对“难民”作出定义)。《移民与国籍法》禁止某些外国人获得庇护,包括实施迫害者、被判犯有特别严重罪行者(包括加重重罪)、在美国境外实施严重非政治性犯罪者、对美国安全构成威胁者、从事某些与恐怖主义相关活动或为恐怖组织成员者,或已在第三国获得确定安置者。[170]
Asylum is a discretionary benefit that can be granted by the Secretary or Attorney General if the alien establishes, among other things, that he or she has experienced past persecution or has a well-founded fear of future persecution on account of race, religion, nationality, membership in a particular social group, or political opinion. INA sec. 208(b)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1158(b)(1) (providing that the Attorney General and Secretary “may” grant asylum to refugees); INA sec. 101(a)(42)(A), 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(42)(A) (defining “refugee”). The INA bars certain aliens from obtaining asylum, including aliens who are persecutors, have been convicted of a particularly serious crime (which includes aggravated felonies), have committed serious nonpolitical crimes outside of the United States, are a danger to the security of the United States, have engaged in certain terrorism-related activities or are members of terrorist organizations, or were firmly resettled in a third country.[170]
《移民与国籍法》还禁止某些外国人申请庇护。[171] 外国人通常必须在其最后一次抵达美国之日起1年内申请庇护。[172] 在1年申请期限届满后申请庇护的外国人不具备申请庇护的资格,除非该外国人证明情况变化实质性地影响了其获得庇护的资格,或存在特殊情况导致其在1年期限内未能及时申请,并且该申请是在情况变化或特殊情况出现后的合理期限内提交的。[173] 即使外国人符合所有庇护条件,包括证实过去遭受迫害或存在对未来迫害的合理恐惧以及符合逾期申请的任何例外情形,部长或司法部长仍可酌情拒绝给予庇护。[174]
The INA also bars certain aliens from applying for asylum.[171] Aliens generally must apply for asylum within 1 year from the date of their last arrival in the United States.[172] An alien who files for asylum after the 1-year filing deadline is not eligible to apply for asylum unless the alien demonstrates that changed circumstances materially affected the alien's eligibility for asylum or extraordinary circumstances delayed filing during the 1-year period, and that the application was filed within a reasonable period of time given the changed or extraordinary circumstances.[173] Even if an alien meets all the criteria for asylum, including establishing past persecution or a well-founded fear of future persecution and any exceptions to late filing, the Secretary or Attorney General can still deny asylum as a matter of discretion.[174]
获准庇护的外国人不得被遣返或遣送回其国籍国或最后惯常居住国,其庇护身份附带工作许可,并且在事先获得部长同意的情况下可获准前往美国境外旅行。[175] 如果外国人在获准庇护时不具备庇护资格,或根据法律规定其后不再具备庇护资格,其庇护身份可被终止。[176]
Aliens who are granted asylum cannot be removed or returned to their country of nationality or last habitual residence, are employment authorized incident to their asylee status, and may be permitted to travel outside of the United States with prior consent from the Secretary.[175] Asylum can be terminated if the alien was not eligible for asylum status at the time of the asylum grant or is otherwise no longer eligible for asylum under the law.[176]
外国人可以将实际身处美国境内的配偶及子女作为受抚养人列入其庇护申请,时间可以是提交申请时,也可以是在该申请作出最终决定之前的任何时间。[177] 该外国人及其受抚养人均被视为庇护申请人,每位申请人均可单独提交(c)(8)类工作许可证(EAD)申请。
Aliens may include their spouse and children who are physically present in the United States as dependents on their asylum application at the time they file or at any time until a final decision is made on the application.[177] The alien and their dependents are considered asylum applicants, and each applicant may individually file an application for a (c)(8) EAD.
2. 主动申请与被动申请
2. Affirmative vs. Defensive Filings
外国人若要申请庇护,须向美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)或移民法院(行政复审执行办公室,EOIR)提交I-589表,《庇护申请及暂缓遣返申请表》。庇护申请根据对该外国人案件具有管辖权的机构不同,分为“主动申请”或“被动申请”。通常,如果外国人身处美国境内、未被拘留且尚未被置于遣返程序中,则该外国人应向USCIS提交庇护申请。此类申请称为“主动申请”。如果美国国土安全部(DHS)已将外国人置于遣返程序中,则该外国人须向移民法官(IJ)提交庇护申请。[178] 此类申请称为“被动申请”,其中包括经USCIS庇护官转交移民法官对其庇护主张进行重新审查(de novo review)的外国人案件。[179]
To request asylum, an alien must file Form I-589, Application for Asylum and for Withholding of Removal, with either USCIS or the immigration court (EOIR). Asylum applications are characterized as “affirmative” or “defensive” based on which agency has jurisdiction over the alien's case. Generally, if an alien is physically present in the United States, not detained, and has not been placed in removal proceedings, the alien files the asylum application with USCIS. These applications are known as “affirmative” filings. If DHS places an alien in removal proceedings, the alien files an application for asylum with an Immigration Judge (IJ).[178] These applications are known as “defensive” filings and include aliens the USCIS asylum officer refers to the IJ for de novo review of their asylum claim.[179]
美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)负责对无人陪伴未成年人(UAC)提交的庇护申请进行初步裁决。这是因为无人陪伴未成年人提交的庇护申请必须按照《预防人口贩运与保护受害者法重新授权法》(TVPRA,公法第110-457号,122 Stat. 5044)以及J.O.P.诉美国国土安全部案(案号8:19-cv-01944,马里兰州地区法院)(2024年11月25日批准)(以下简称“J.O.P.和解协议”)中确立的和解协议所规定的要求进行处理。适用于无人陪伴未成年人的TVPRA条款自2009年3月23日起生效,赋予USCIS对无人陪伴未成年人提交的所有庇护申请的初始管辖权。因此,即使无人陪伴未成年人已被移民法院签发出庭通知书(Notice to Appear),只要其在首次提交庇护申请之日为无人陪伴未成年人,其庇护申请仍可由USCIS受理审理。TVPRA还为那些此前未向USCIS提交庇护申请、且在移民法院、移民上诉委员会(Board of Immigration Appeals)上诉程序中或联邦法院有未决申请的无人陪伴未成年人提供了机会,使其庇护申请可由USCIS庇护官在非对抗性环境中受理并作出裁决。[180]此外,根据J.O.P.和解协议的条款,USCIS不会依赖美国司法部(DOJ)关于某外国人不属于无人陪伴未成年人的任何认定。[181]相反,USCIS对无人陪伴未成年人庇护申请的裁决行使初始管辖权,并作出自己的管辖权认定。[182]因此,如果无人陪伴未成年人的(第8647页)庇护申请仍在USCIS处于未决状态,即使其庇护申请已被移民法官(IJ)、移民上诉委员会(BIA)或联邦法院驳回,其依据(c)(8)条款获得的工作许可文件(EAD)也不会自动终止。
USCIS is responsible for initial adjudication of asylum applications filed by UACs. This is because an asylum application filed by a UAC must be processed according to requirements established in the TVPRA, Public Law 110-457, 122 Stat. 5044, and the settlement agreement in J.O.P. v. U.S. Dept of Homeland Security, 8:19-cv-01944 (D. Md.) (approved Nov. 25, 2024) (J.O.P. Settlement Agreement). The provisions of the TVPRA that apply to UACs took effect on March 23, 2009 and provide USCIS with initial jurisdiction over all asylum applications filed by UACs. Thus, even UACs who have been issued a Notice to Appear in immigration court can have their application for asylum heard by USCIS if they were UACs on the date they first filed for asylum. The TVPRA also provides an opportunity for UACs, who did not previously file for asylum with USCIS and who had a pending claim in immigration court, on appeal to the Board of Immigration Appeals, or in federal court, to have their asylum claim heard and adjudicated by a USCIS Asylum Officer in a non-adversarial setting.[180] Furthermore, under the terms of the J.O.P. Settlement Agreement, USCIS will not rely on any determination by DOJ that an alien is not a UAC.[181] Rather, USCIS exercises initial jurisdiction over the adjudication of the UAC's asylum application and renders its own jurisdictional determination.[182] Therefore, if a UAC's ( printed page 8647) pending asylum application remains pending before USCIS, his or her (c)(8) EAD will not automatically terminate even if his or her asylum application was denied by an IJ, BIA, or a Federal court.
在美国口岸(空港、海港或陆路口岸)出示身份以求入境的外国人通常被视为入境申请人。[183] 《移民与国籍法》(INA)第235(a)(1)条,《美国法典》第8编第1225(a)(1)节。如果移民官认定某外国人根据该法第212(a)(6)(C)条或第212(a)(7)条(《美国法典》第8编第1182(a)(6)(C)节或第1182(a)(7)节)因持有虚假文件、作出虚假陈述或缺乏所需旅行证件而不可入境,则该外国人可能依据该法第235(b)(1)条(《美国法典》第8编第1225(b)(1)节)被置于快速遣返程序。快速遣返程序也可适用于某些其他未被准许入境或假释进入美国、且无法证明在作出不可入境认定之日前两年内持续在美国实际居住的外国人。《移民与国籍法》第235(b)(1)(A)(iii)条,《美国法典》第8编第1225(b)(1)(A)(iii)节。处于快速遣返程序中的外国人,若表明有意申请庇护、表达对迫害或酷刑的恐惧,或表达对遣返回原籍国的恐惧,将被转介给庇护官,以确定该外国人是否具有可信的恐惧。[184]《移民与国籍法》第235(b)(1)条,《美国法典》第8编第1225(b)(1)节;《联邦法规汇编》第8编第208.30(b)节;第235.3(b)(4)节。如果认定某外国人具有可信的恐惧,“该外国人应被拘留,以便进一步审理其庇护申请。”《移民与国籍法》第235(b)(1)(B)(ii)条,《美国法典》第8编第1225(b)(1)(B)(ii)节。
Aliens who present themselves at a U.S. port of entry (air, sea, or land) are generally deemed applicants for admission.[183] INA sec. 235(a)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1225(a)(1). If an immigration officer determines that an alien is inadmissible under section 212(a)(6)(C) or 212(a)(7) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1182(a)(6)(C) or 1182(a)(7), for being in possession of false documents, making false statements, or lacking the required travel documentation, the alien may be placed in expedited removal proceedings under section 235(b)(1) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1225(b)(1). Expedited removal may also be applied to certain other aliens who have not been admitted or paroled into the United States and who cannot show that they have been continuously physically present in the United States for the two years prior to the date of determination of inadmissibility. INA sec. 235(b)(1)(A)(iii), 8 U.S.C. 1225(b)(1)(A)(iii). Aliens in expedited removal proceedings who indicate an intention to apply for asylum, express a fear of persecution or torture, or a fear of return to their home country are referred to an asylum officer to determine whether the alien has a credible fear of persecution or torture.[184] INA sec. 235(b)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1225(b)(1); 8 CFR 208.30(b); 8 CFR 235.3(b)(4). If an alien is determined to have a credible fear, “the alien shall be detained for further consideration of the application for asylum.” INA sec. 235(b)(1)(B)(ii), 8 U.S.C. 1225(b)(1)(B)(ii).
最初基于可信恐惧肯定性认定的庇护申请,一旦向法院提交出庭通知书(NTA),即归移民法院执行办公室(EOIR)管辖,并被视为“防御性提交”的申请。同样地,如果某外国人获得可信恐惧的肯定性认定,但被美国移民及海关执法局(ICE)从拘留中释放,该外国人在出庭通知书提交后仍被视为归EOIR管辖,并必须向法院提交庇护申请。
Asylum applications based initially on a positive credible fear determination are under the jurisdiction of EOIR once a Notice to Appear (NTA) is filed with the court and are considered “defensively filed” applications. Similarly, if an alien has a positive credible fear determination, but is released from detention by U.S. Immigration and Customs Enforcement (ICE), the alien is still considered to be under EOIR jurisdiction once the NTA is filed and must file the application for asylum with the court.
3. 庇护申请人的工作许可
3. Employment Authorization for Asylum Applicants
外国人可根据其移民身份或法律或法规规定的其他条件获准在美国工作。见《联邦法规汇编》第8编第274a.12节。庇护申请人并不因法律规定而自动有权获得工作许可,但国会授权部长可通过法规,在庇护申请审理期间酌情批准此类外国人的工作许可。见《移民与国籍法》第208(d)(2)条,《美国法典》第8编第1158(d)(2)节。寻求工作许可的外国人通常必须按照表格说明,向USCIS提交I-765表格申请工作许可文件(EAD),并缴纳规定的费用。《联邦法规汇编》第8编第274a.13(a)节。第8编第208.7节和第274a.12(c)(8)节规定了庇护申请人工作许可的相关规定。
An alien may be authorized for employment in the United States based on the alien's immigration status or other conditions, as established by statute or by regulation. See8 CFR 274a.12. An asylum applicant is not entitled to employment authorization by statute, but Congress granted the Secretary discretion to authorize employment, through regulations, for these aliens while the asylum application is pending adjudication. See INA sec. 208(d)(2), 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2). Aliens seeking employment authorization generally must apply for an EAD by filing Form I-765 with USCIS in accordance with the form instructions, along with any prescribed fee. 8 CFR 274a.13(a). The regulations at 8 CFR 208.7 and 274a.12(c)(8) govern employment authorization for asylum applicants.
a. 180天庇护EAD时钟
a. 180-Day Asylum EAD Clock
根据现行法律法规,国土安全部长在庇护申请提交之日起180天内不得向庇护申请人授予工作许可。《移民与国籍法》(INA)第208(d)(2)条,8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2),8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)。这一180天期限通常被称为“180天庇护工作许可(EAD)时钟”。[185] 180天庇护工作许可时钟在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)或行政复议办公室(EOIR,视情况而定)受理庇护申请进行处理后开始计算。8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)。现行法规规定,USCIS或EOIR应在收到申请后30天内将不完整的申请退回外国人,但如果USCIS或EOIR在该时限内未退回不完整的庇护申请,则该申请自动被视为完整。8 CFR 208.3(c)、1208.3(c)(3)(现行有效版本)。[186] 庇护申请一经受理,外国人必须等待150天后才可提交工作许可申请。8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)。USCIS自工作许可(EAD)申请提交之日起有30天的时间对该申请进行裁决。同上。因此,180天庇护工作许可时钟包括提交(c)(8)类工作许可申请前的150天等待期,以及USCIS裁决工作许可申请所额外拥有的30天期限。
Under the current statute and regulations, the Secretary cannot grant employment authorization to an asylum applicant until 180 days after the filing of the asylum application. INA sec. 208(d)(2), 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(2), 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1). This 180-day period is commonly called the “180-day Asylum EAD Clock.” [185] The 180-day Asylum EAD Clock begins to run after USCIS or EOIR, as applicable, accepts the asylum application for processing. 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1). Existing regulations provide that USCIS or EOIR should return an incomplete application to the alien within 30 days of receipt of the application, but if USCIS or EOIR has not returned the incomplete asylum application within that time, the application is automatically deemed complete. 8 CFR 208.3(c), 1208.3(c)(3) (as effective).[186] Once the asylum application is accepted, the alien must wait 150 days before they may file the application for employment authorization. 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1). USCIS has 30 days from the filing date of the EAD application to adjudicate the application. Id. The 180-day Asylum EAD Clock therefore includes the 150-day waiting period for filing the (c)(8) EAD application and the additional 30-day period that USCIS has to adjudicate the EAD application.
由外国人提出请求或造成的延误会使180天庇护工作许可时钟停止计算,并且在外国人消除该延误之前,或直到下一次预定的案件事项(例如重新安排的面谈或延期的听证会)发生之前,该时钟不会重新开始计算。8 CFR 208.7(a)(2)。例如,如果外国人未能出席其庇护申请所要求的生物识别信息采集预约,180天庇护工作许可时钟将停止,直至该外国人出席其生物识别信息采集预约后方可重新计算。同上。同样,如果外国人要求修改或补充其庇护申请、未能在庇护面谈时提供合格的口译人员,或将庇护面谈改期至较晚日期,所有这些行为均会使180天庇护工作许可时钟停止计算,且该时钟在所要求的行动完成之前不会重新开始计算。[187] 8 CFR 208.7(a)(2)。如果在USCIS裁决初次(c)(8)类工作许可申请时,庇护申请仍存在尚未解决的、由外国人造成的延误,导致该外国人无法累计满180天,USCIS将拒绝该工作许可申请。[188] 因此,部分庇护申请人在获得工作许可之前可能需要等待超过180天的时间。
Delays requested or caused by the alien stop the 180-day Asylum EAD Clock, and it does not run again until the alien cures the delay or until the next scheduled case event, such as a rescheduled interview or a continued hearing. 8 CFR 208.7(a)(2). For example, if an alien fails to appear for a required biometrics appointment on their asylum application, the 180-day Asylum EAD Clock will stop and not recommence until the alien appears for his or her biometrics appointment. Id. Similarly, if an alien asks to amend or supplement his or her asylum application, fails to provide a competent interpreter at the asylum interview, or reschedules the asylum interview for a later date, all of these actions will stop the 180-day Asylum EAD Clock, and the clock will not restart until the required action is completed.[187] 8 CFR 208.7(a)(2). USCIS will deny an EAD application if the asylum application is still subject to an unresolved alien-caused delay that prevents the alien from accumulating 180 days at the time USCIS adjudicates the initial (c)(8) EAD application.[188] As a result, some asylum applicants may wait longer than 180 days before they can be granted employment authorization.
b. 30天处理时限
b. 30-Day Processing Timeframe
根据8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)现行法规,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)必须在外国人提交I-765表格后30天内,就(c)(8)类别的初次就业授权申请作出裁决。[189]8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)规定的30天处理时限是在25多年前确立的,当时前移民归化局(INS)在INS地方办事处审理EAD申请,但现在EAD申请由USCIS服务中心审理。(第8648页打印版)由于多种因素的共同作用,包括EAD申请量的大幅增长、应用服务中心(ASC)预约安排的需要,[190]对更多国家安全和公共安全问题的识别,以及欺诈问题的程度和复杂性的增加,USCIS无法使裁决速度与收案量相匹配。因此,2015年5月22日,Rosario诉USCIS案(案号C15-0813JLR,华盛顿州西区地方法院)的原告在美国华盛顿州西区地方法院提起集体诉讼,要求USCIS遵守8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)规定的30天处理时限。[191]2018年7月26日,法院发布禁令,禁止USCIS继续未能遵守EAD申请裁决的30天处理时限。截至2025年3月,USCIS在30天内完成了86.4%的初次(c)(8)类EAD申请,在60天内完成了98.3%的初次(c)(8)类申请。然而,遵守该法院命令给本已有限的机构资源带来了巨大压力,尤其是考虑到初次(c)(8)类EAD申请(根据特殊ABC程序提交的申请除外)是免费的,如果不增加额外的机构资源或对其他申请、请愿书和福利请求(包括其他EAD类别)的处理时间产生负面影响,USCIS将无法长期持续承担这一负担。为跟上初次(c)(8)类EAD申请收案量的急剧增长,分配给初次(c)(8)类EAD申请的全职等效官员工时已从约50小时增加到2025年3月创下的超过800小时的最高水平。鉴于此前初次(c)(8)类EAD申请(根据特殊ABC程序提交的申请除外)不收取费用,受理、裁决、客户服务及其他支持职能的成本历来由支付费用的其他福利申请人承担。
Under current regulations at 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1), USCIS must adjudicate initial employment authorization applications under the (c)(8) category within 30 days of when the alien files the Form I-765.[189] The 30-day ( printed page 8648) processing timeframe in 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1) was established more than 25 years ago, when the former INS adjudicated EAD applications at local INS offices, but EAD applications are now adjudicated at USCIS Service Centers. As a result of numerous factors, including a massive growth in EAD application volume, the need for ASC appointment scheduling,[190] the identification of more national security and public safety concerns, and an increase in the level and complexity of fraud concerns, USCIS was unable to match the pace of adjudications to the volume of receipts. As result, on May 22, 2015, plaintiffs in Rosario v. USCIS, No. C15-0813JLR (W.D. Wash.), brought a class action in the U.S. District Court for the Western District of Washington to compel USCIS to comply with the 30-day processing timeframe of 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1).[191] On July 26, 2018, the court enjoined USCIS from further failing to adhere to the 30-day processing timeframe for adjudicating EAD applications. As of March 2025, USCIS completed 86.4 percent of initial (c)(8) EAD applications within 30 days and completed 98.3 percent of initial (c)(8) applications within 60 days. However, compliance with the court order places significant strain on already limited agency resources, especially considering that initial (c)(8) EAD applications (except those filed under the special ABC procedures) are free of cost, and USCIS will not be able to continue to sustain this burden in the long-term without adding additional agency resources or negatively impacting processing times for other applications, petitions, and benefit requests, including other EAD categories. Full-time equivalent officer hours allocated to initial (c)(8) EAD applications have increased from approximately 50 to a high-water mark of over 800 in March of 2025 in order to keep pace with the drastic increase in initial (c)(8) EAD application receipts. Given that there was previously no fee for initial (c)(8) EAD applications (except those filed under the special ABC procedures), the costs of intake, adjudication, and customer service and other support functions were historically borne by other benefit requestors who pay fees.
c. 拒绝庇护申请对就业授权的影响
c. Impact of Denial of the Asylum Application on Employment Authorization
拒绝庇护申请会以不同方式影响外国人申请和保留就业授权的能力,具体取决于拒绝发生的时间和地点。
Denial of the asylum application impacts the alien's ability to apply for and retain employment authorization in different ways, depending on when and where the denial occurred.
如果庇护申请在申请庇护后150天等待期内被庇护官员或移民法官(IJ)拒绝,则外国人不得申请就业授权。见8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)。如果就业授权申请是在150天等待期之后提交的,且庇护申请在就业授权申请裁决之前被拒绝,则就业授权申请将被拒绝。同上。
If the asylum application is denied by an asylum officer or IJ within the 150-day waiting period after applying for asylum, the alien may not apply for employment authorization. 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1). If the application for employment authorization is filed after the 150-day waiting period and the asylum application is denied prior to adjudication of the application for employment authorization, employment authorization will be denied. Id.
如果外国人基于待决的主动申请庇护案件申请并获得EAD,而庇护申请随后被庇护官员拒绝,则该EAD将在其到期日终止,或在庇护申请被拒绝后60天终止,以较晚者为准。见8 CFR 208.7(b)(1)。如果外国人获得EAD后,庇护申请随后被USCIS移交给美国移民法院执行办公室(EOIR),则就业授权在EAD到期日之前仍然有效。见8 CFR 208.7(b)(2)。
If the alien applies for and is granted an EAD based on a pending affirmative asylum application and the asylum application is denied by the asylum officer, the EAD will either terminate on its expiration date or 60 days after the denial of the asylum application, whichever is later. 8 CFR 208.7(b)(1). If the alien receives an EAD and the asylum application is later referred by USCIS to EOIR, employment authorization will remain valid through the expiration date on the EAD. 8 CFR 208.7(b)(2).
如果移民法官、移民上诉委员会(BIA)或联邦法院拒绝庇护申请,且外国人未提出相应的行政复审或司法复审请求,则就业授权将在EAD上印制的日期到期。见8 CFR 208.7(b)(2)。如果移民法官、移民上诉委员会或联邦法院拒绝庇护申请,而外国人选择提出相应的行政复审或司法复审请求,则就业授权在EAD到期日之前仍然有效,外国人有资格在其到期时申请续签EAD。见8 CFR 208.7(c)。
If the IJ, BIA, or Federal court denies the asylum application and the alien does not file the appropriate request for administrative or judicial review, employment authorization will expire on the date printed on the EAD. 8 CFR 208.7(b)(2). If the IJ, BIA, or Federal court denies the asylum application and the alien chooses to file the appropriate request for administrative or judicial review, employment authorization will remain valid through the EAD expiration date, and the alien will be eligible to file for a renewal EAD upon its expiration. 8 CFR 208.7(c).
IV. 相关规则制定
IV. Related Rulemaking
与本规则同时,国土安全部(DHS)正在开展其他处于不同制定阶段的规则制定行动。国土安全部已就这些其他规则对本规则可能产生的外围性、重叠性或相互关联性影响进行了考量和分析,并已将其影响(如有)纳入本拟议规则的支持性文件、政策和法规文本之中。
Simultaneously with this rule, DHS is engaging in other rulemaking actions that are in various stages of development. DHS has considered and analyzed these other rules for peripheral, overlapping, or interrelated effects on this rule and has incorporated their effects, if any, into the supporting documentation, policies, and regulatory text for this proposed rule.
A. 自由裁量性EAD公告拟议规则制定(NPRM)
A. Discretionary EAD NPRM
在另一份单独的拟议规则制定公告(NPRM)中,国土安全部拟对管辖以下外国人自由裁量性工作许可的相关法规进行修订,此类外国人包括:已被作出最终驱逐令,但因受监管令(OSUP)而暂时获释不予羁押的外国人;因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益而被临时假释入境美国的外国人;或已获准延期执行行动的外国人。国土安全部拟对申请上述各类自由裁量性工作许可的资格条件予以限制和明确。国土安全部进一步拟明确规定,申请自由裁量性工作许可的外国人若符合以下情形,通常不应获得有利的自由裁量,除非存在重大的相抵公共利益:(1)承认实施过暴力或危险犯罪行为的外国人,即使该人从未被正式逮捕、起诉、大陪审团起诉或定罪;(2)曾因任何犯罪行为被逮捕、被起诉(无论是否作出处理结果)、被大陪审团起诉或被定罪的外国人;或(3)有证据表明该外国人为帮派或恐怖组织成员的外国人。国土安全部指出,该拟议规则将列入可公开查阅的《2025年秋季联邦监管与放松监管行动统一议程》。
In a separate notice of proposed rulemaking (NPRM), DHS is will propose amendments to regulations governing discretionary employment authorization for certain aliens who: have final orders of removal, but are temporarily released from custody on orders of supervision (OSUP); are paroled into the United States temporarily for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit; or have been granted deferred action. DHS proposes to limit and clarify eligibility to apply for these categories of discretionary employment authorization. DHS further proposes to specify that aliens applying for discretionary employment authorization: (1) who admit committing a violent or dangerous crime even if he or she has never been formally arrested, charged, indicted, or convicted; (2) who have been arrested for, charged with (without disposition), indicted for, or convicted of any criminal acts; or (3) for whom there is evidence of the alien's membership in a gang or terrorist organization, generally do not warrant a favorable exercise of discretion unless there are significant countervailing public interests. DHS notes that this proposed rule will be listed in the publicly available Fall 2025 Unified Agenda of Federal Regulatory and Deregulatory Actions.
国土安全部已就本《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》与《自由裁量性EAD拟议规则制定公告》可能产生的综合影响进行了考量。由于《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》与《自由裁量性EAD拟议规则制定公告》中提出的部分修订内容大体上存在重叠,两项拟议规则之间类似修订内容的并存,总体上减轻了综合影响。尽管《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》确实与《自由裁量性EAD拟议规则制定公告》存在交叉之处,但国土安全部是以现行法规文本作为修订依据的,因为《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》在此阶段提出的任何修订均仅属拟议性质,尚未定案。此外,虽然《自由裁量性EAD拟议规则制定公告》将涉及对8 CFR 274a.12(c)项下所有工作许可相关内容的修订,但不会涉及对8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8)的任何修订。这将使《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》能够全面处理(c)(8)项下的相关问题,而《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》不会对《自由裁量性EAD拟议规则制定公告》所涉及的类别作出修订。国土安全部承认,若《自由裁量性EAD最终规则》在《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》之前生效,则可能(印刷页第8649页)有必要对相应的法规文本进行修订,以反映《自由裁量性EAD最终规则》中所作的相应变更。
DHS considered the possible combined effects of this Asylum EAD Reform NPRM and the Discretionary EAD NPRM. As some of the proposed amendments made in the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM and the Discretionary EAD NPRM generally overlap, the combined effects are generally mitigated by the inclusion of similar amendments between the two proposed rules. While the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM does intersect with the Discretionary EAD NPRM, DHS is using current regulatory text as the basis for changes, as any changes proposed by the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM at this point in the process are just that—proposed. Further, while the Discretionary EAD NPRM will include changes related to all employment authorization under 8 CFR 274a.12(c), it will not include any changes to 8 CFR 274a.12(c)(8). This will allow the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM to fully address (c)(8) issues, and the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM will not make changes to categories addressed in the Discretionary EAD NPRM. DHS acknowledges that, if the Discretionary EAD Final Rule goes into effect prior to the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM, it may ( printed page 8649) be necessary to amend the appropriate regulatory text to reflect the corresponding changes in the Discretionary EAD Final Rule.
B. 生物识别信息拟议规则制定公告(NPRM)
B. Biometrics NPRM
在另一项单独的规则制定行动中,国土安全部拟对国土安全部关于生物识别信息使用与采集的相关法规进行修订。国土安全部将拟议,除其他事项外,更新生物识别信息的法规定义,以确保其涵盖已获认可的识别方式,并扩大须提交生物识别信息的人员范围。就本《庇护EAD改革拟议规则制定公告》而言,该公告拟对申请(c)(8)类工作许可的庇护申请人设定一般性生物识别信息提交要求,与此相关的是,《生物识别信息拟议规则制定公告》将拟议要求所有提交移民或入籍利益申请、其他申请或信息收集请求的个人,或与之相关联的个人,均须提交生物识别信息,除非国土安全部对该要求予以豁免。国土安全部提出上述修订,旨在加强整个移民生命周期内身份核验与管理的准确性。该拟议修订还将增强国土安全部识别和遏制移民福利欺诈的能力,并使国土安全部能够在处理移民福利申请时开展更全面的基于生物识别信息的背景审查。提交生物识别信息的外国人将承担与时间和出行相关的费用。相关费用已在经济分析中详细说明,但国土安全部未对其总体货币化影响作出估算。国土安全部指出,该拟议规则将列入可公开查阅的《2025年秋季联邦监管与放松监管行动统一议程》。
In another separate rulemaking, DHS is proposing to amend DHS regulations governing the use and collection of biometrics by DHS. DHS will propose, among other things, updates to the regulatory definition of biometrics to ensure it captures accepted modalities and to expand the population of individuals required to submit biometrics. As relevant to this Asylum EAD Reform NPRM, which would establish a general biometrics requirement for asylum applicants seeking a (c)(8) EAD, the Biometrics NPRM will propose to require biometrics from all individuals filing for, or associated with, an immigration or naturalization benefit request, other request, or collection of information, unless DHS exempts the requirement DHS is proposing these changes to enhance accurate identity verification and management throughout the immigration lifecycle. The proposed changes would also enhance DHS's ability to identify and deter immigration benefits fraud, and allow DHS to perform more comprehensive biometrics-based background checks in connection with immigration benefits requests. Aliens who submit biometrics would face costs associated with time and travel. These are detailed in the economic analysis, but DHS does not estimate the total monetized impact. DHS notes that this proposed rule will be listed in the publicly available Fall 2025 Unified Agenda of Federal Regulatory and Deregulatory Actions.
国土安全部（DHS）考虑了庇护就业许可文件改革拟议规则制定通知（Asylum EAD Reform NPRM）与生物识别拟议规则制定通知（Biometrics NPRM）可能产生的综合影响。由于庇护就业许可文件改革拟议规则制定通知与生物识别拟议规则制定通知中所作的至少一项修订内容大体重叠，这两项拟议规则之间类似修订内容的纳入通常缓解了综合影响。具体而言，国土安全部在庇护就业许可文件拟议规则制定通知和生物识别拟议规则制定通知中均提议要求所有(c)(8)类就业许可文件申请人提交生物识别信息。
DHS considered the possible combined effects of Asylum EAD Reform NPRM and the Biometrics NPRM. As at least one of the amendments made in the Asylum EAD Reform NPRM and the Biometrics NPRM generally overlap, the combined effects are generally mitigated by the inclusion of a similar amendment between the two proposed rules. Specifically, DHS is proposing to require biometrics for all (c)(8) EAD applicants in both the Asylum EAD NPRM and the Biometrics NPRM.
V. 拟议规则的讨论
V. Discussion of Proposed Rule
A. 暂停并重新启动(c)(8)类就业许可文件申请的受理
A. Pause and Re-Start of (c)(8) EAD Application Acceptance
《1996年非法移民改革和移民责任法》（IIRIRA）修订了该法案，规定根据该法案第208(d)(1)条（8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1)）建立的任何庇护程序“应规定……，在无特殊情况下，[庇护]申请的最终行政裁决……应在申请提交之日起180天内完成。”[192]1996年，国会决定力求在申请提交后180天内完成初审庇护申请的裁决。当时国会和政府双方都为实现该处理目标投入了大量资源。[193]在1994年和1996年的法规及条例变更之后，新的庇护申请数量从1995财年的峰值154,464件下降至1999财年的32,711件。[194]由于改革和资源增加的双重作用，庇护制度在保护与福利完整性两方面都更接近实现目标，也更接近庇护程序整体的初衷。[195]该初衷一直是，一旦提出庇护申请，应及时作出裁决，从而无需在外国人收到庇护申请裁决之前颁发就业许可文件。
The IIRIRA amended the Act to state that any asylum procedures established under section 208(d)(1) of the Act, 8 U.S.C. 1158(d)(1), “shall provide that . . . , in the absence of exceptional circumstances, final administrative adjudication of [an] asylum application . . . shall be completed within 180 days after the date an application is filed.” [192] In 1996, Congress decided to pursue completion of first-instance asylum application decisions within 180 days of filing. Both Congress and the administration at that time provided significant resources to accomplish that processing goal.[193] After the statutory and regulatory changes of 1994 and 1996, new asylum filings decreased from their peak of 154,464 in FY 1995 to 32,711 in FY 1999.[194] As a result of both the reforms and the increase in resources, the asylum system moved closer to accomplishing both protection and benefit integrity, and closer to aligning with the original intent behind the asylum process as a whole.[195] The intent has always been that once an asylum claim is filed, a decision is made in a timely manner so that there is no need for an employment authorization document until the alien has received a decision on the asylum application.
如今，国土安全部再次发现庇护制度资源不足，被庇护申请压得不堪重负，因此很容易成为许多人提交轻率、欺诈或其他无实质内容的庇护申请以获取就业许可的目标。[196]近年来，美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）在积案式庇护申请受理和可信恐惧审查方面均不堪重负，导致积案增加。2022年，美国公民及移民服务局收到247,790件积案式庇护申请，2023年收到464,398件积案式庇护申请，几乎是2022年受理量的两倍。[197]近年来，可信恐惧案件量也大幅增加，从2009年的5,216件低点增至2019年的103,295件。[198]2022年，美国公民及移民服务局完成了54,092件可信恐惧案件。[199]2023年，该数字几乎增至三倍，达到150,431件可信恐惧受理案件。[200]2023年，随着《第42条》的失效，美国公民及移民服务局将90%以上的庇护官员分配用于处理预期激增的可信恐惧案件。[201]这使得只有约3%的庇护官员负责审理积案式庇护案件，从而使积案持续增长。（印刷页第8650页）[202]与积案式庇护项目类似，行政法官及移民上诉办公室（EOIR）的庇护申请受理量也增长了两倍多，从2022财年的265,632件增至2024财年的905,632件。[203]尽管2024财年完成的积案式庇护案件平均处理时间为1,287天，但值得注意的是，这一数字涵盖了所有积案式庇护案件，包括积压案件、后进先出（LIFO）案件，以及任何被优先审理的案件，例如阿富汗“盟友欢迎行动”（OAW）案件、[204]强制令案件和加急案件。2025财年第一季度，新提交积案式庇护申请的申请人预计处理时间为765.75个月，即超过63年；而2025财年第二季度的新申请人，美国公民及移民服务局预计处理时间约为562.25个月，即超过46年。[205]国土安全部认为，当前庇护申请的数量和处理时间反映出与改革前申请情况相似的动态，而此前改革的效果也支持这样的推断，即存在大量仅为获取就业许可而提交的轻率、欺诈或其他无实质内容的庇护申请。庇护制度再次需要进行改革，使就业许可与庇护申请的提交脱钩。然而，目前的情况已演变为灾难性局面，需要采取与问题严重程度相匹配的新颖解决方案。
Yet again, DHS again finds the asylum system to be under-resourced and overwhelmed with asylum applications, and consequently an easy target for many driven by the opportunity to receive employment authorization by filing a frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum application.[196] In recent years, USCIS has been overwhelmed by both affirmative asylum receipts and credible fear screenings, leading to an increase in the backlog. In 2022, USCIS received 247,790 affirmative asylum receipts, and in 2023 received 464,398 affirmative asylum receipts, nearly double the 2022 receipts.[197] Over recent years, the credible fear caseload has also significantly increased, going from a low of 5,216 cases in 2009 to 103,295 cases in 2019.[198] In 2022, USCIS completed 54,092 credible fear cases.[199] In 2023, that number almost tripled to 150,431 credible fear receipts.[200] In 2023, with the expiration of Title 42, USCIS allocated more than 90% of its asylum officers to process an expected surge of credible fear cases.[201] This left only about 3 percent of asylum officers to adjudicate affirmative asylum cases, thus allowing the backlog to continue to ( printed page 8650) grow.[202] Similar to the affirmative asylum program, EOIR also more than tripled their asylum application receipts, going from 265,632 in FY 2022 to 905,632 in FY 2024.[203] While the average processing time for an affirmative asylum case completed in FY 2024 was 1,287 days, it is important to note this includes the universe of affirmative asylum cases, including backlog, LIFO, and any case prioritized for adjudication, such as Afghan Operation Allies Welcome (OAW),[204] mandamus, and expedited cases. In FY 2025 Q1, new affirmative asylum applicants could expect processing to take 765.75 months, or more than 63 years; and for new filers in FY 2025 Q2, USCIS expects processing to take approximately 562.25 months, or more than 46 years.[205] DHS believes the current volume and processing times of asylum applications reflects similar dynamics as the pre-reform filings, and the effect of the prior reform supports the deduction that there are many frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum application filings that are filed solely for the purposes of obtaining an EAD. The asylum system is again in need of a reform that decouples employment authorization from the filing of an asylum application. However, the situation has now turned catastrophic and requires novel solutions that meet the severity of the problem.
如果最终确定,当国土安全部(DHS)连续90天内的积极庇护申请平均处理时间[206]超过180天时,国土安全部将暂停受理初次(c)(8)类工作许可文件(EAD)申请。[207]当连续90天内积极庇护裁定的平均处理时间小于或等于180天时,将恢复受理初次(c)(8)类工作许可文件申请。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)局长关于暂停或恢复受理(c)(8)类工作许可文件申请的决定不具有自由裁量性质,该决定将直接与前90天内所有积极庇护申请的处理时间挂钩。国土安全部承认,暂停受理初次(c)(8)类工作许可文件申请将对庇护申请人造成潜在的重大困难。根据未来庇护申请接收量的变化情况(如果本规则按提议最终确定,接收量可能会减少,但也会因其他各种因素而继续发生变化),初始暂停可能会持续相当长的时间。工作许可文件申请受理和处理的暂停可能持续14年至173年,甚至更长时间。例如,在不考虑本规则中任何其他拟议变更及其对裁定影响的情况下,如果接收量减少80%(如1994年法规改革后的情况),美国公民及移民服务局可能需要长达14年的时间才能达到180天的处理时间。[208]相反,如果接收量减少50%,美国公民及移民服务局可能需要长达173年的时间才能达到180天的处理时间。需要再次强调的是,这两项预测均未考虑本规则中任何其他拟议变更,而这些变更如果最终确定,也将缩短这些处理时间。[209]美国公民及移民服务局认识到,这种暂停的效果将是在较长时间内限制新申请人获得基于待决庇护申请的工作许可,暂停的持续时间取决于未来庇护申请接收量的减少程度。尽管这是对基于待决庇护申请获得工作许可的重大改变,国土安全部认为有必要行使其法定自由裁量权来实施这些变更,以实现其加强福利诚信、保护国家安全和减轻资源压力的目标。
If finalized, DHS would pause the acceptance of initial (c)(8) EAD applications when the average processing time [206] for affirmative asylum applications over a consecutive period of 90 days adjudication exceeds 180 days.[207] Acceptance of initial (c)(8) EAD applications would resume when the average processing time for affirmative asylum adjudications over a consecutive period of 90 days is less than or equal to 180 days. The USCIS Director's determination to pause or restart acceptance of (c)(8) EAD applications is not discretionary, and that determination would be directly tethered to the processing times of all affirmative asylum applications over the previous 90-day period. DHS acknowledges that the pausing of acceptance of initial (c)(8) EAD applications will create a potentially significant hardship for asylum applicants. Depending on asylum receipts moving forward, which will likely decrease if this rule is finalized as proposed but will also continue to be subject to change due to a variety of other factors, the initial pause may last a significant amount of time. The pause on EAD application acceptances and processing may last from 14 to 173 years, or longer. For example, without factoring in any of the other proposed changes in this rule and how they would impact adjudications, if receipts decrease by 80 percent, as they did following the 1994 regulatory reforms, it could take USCIS as long as 14 years to reach a 180-day processing time.[208] If, instead, receipts decrease by 50 percent, it could take USCIS as long as 173 years to reach a 180-day processing time. It bears repeating that neither of those projections take into account any of the other proposed changes in this rule which, if finalized, would also shorten those processing times.[209] USCIS recognizes that the effect of this pause would be to restrict access to pending asylum application-based employment authorization for new applicants for an extended period, with the duration of the pause determined by the future decrease in asylum application receipts. While this is a significant change in access to pending asylum application-based employment authorization, DHS believes it is necessary to exercise its statutory discretion to implement these changes to achieve its goals of enhancing benefit integrity, protecting national security, and reducing resource strains.
如本规则前文多处所述,国土安全部面临着与20世纪90年代初移民归化局(INS)类似的局面。移民归化局当时采取了某些法规改革措施,成功遏制了无实质理由的申请,并对庇护案件作出了公正及时的裁定。[210]在这些庇护改革之后,新的庇护申请量实际上从1995财年的峰值149,566件下降到1999财年的仅30,261件,在短短五个财年内下降了近80%。[211]与此同时,批准率显著提高,从1993财年裁定案件中的15%提高到1999财年的38%。[212]因此,移民归化局的改革达成了该规则制定的既定目标,即防止外国人主要为获得工作许可而申请庇护,同时使移民归化局能够更迅速地向那些确实应得此项救济的人授予庇护,并提供工作许可”。[213]由于本规则制定中的提议旨在产生与移民归化局1994年实施的改革类似的效果,国土安全部预计本规则制定最终将取得与移民归化局所取得的类似成果。
As discussed in several places earlier in this rule, DHS is confronted with a similar situation to the INS in the early 1990s. The INS responded with certain regulatory reforms that succeeded in curtailing meritless claims and delivering fair and timely decisions on asylum cases.[210] In the wake of those asylum reforms, new asylum filings actually decreased from their then-peak of 149,566 in FY 1995 to just 30,261 in FY 1999, a decrease of nearly 80 percent in only five FYs.[211] At the same time, the approval rate significantly increased, from 15 percent of cases adjudicated in FY 1993 to 38 percent in FY 1999.[212] Consequently, INS's reforms met the stated goals of that rulemaking, preventing aliens from applying for asylum primarily as a means to obtain employment authorization, while simultaneously enabling the INS to more promptly grant asylum—and provide work authorization—to those who merit this relief”.[213] Because the proposals in this rulemaking are designed to have a similar effect to those reforms implemented by the INS in 1994, DHS expects this rulemaking will eventually achieve similar results to those achieved by the INS.
如上文所述,美国国土安全部(DHS)主要试图解决围绕庇护积压案件的问题,但由于诸多运营方面的关切和相互竞争的裁决优先事项,甚至难以着手处理这些案件(印刷页8651)。DHS可以从情况角度证明,这些拟议改革将有助于DHS实现其既定目标,而这些目标与移民归化局(INS)1994年的目标相同。出于假设分析的目的,假设DHS发布的最终规则与此处的拟议规则保持一致,并取得与INS在1994年所取得的类似成果,即在短短五个财政年度内使庇护申请减少近80%,那么DHS届时即可重新调配庇护资源,更成功地应对迫在眉睫的积压案件。DHS还可以将其就业授权文件(EAD)裁决资源转移,以支持及时裁决初次(c)(8)类EAD申请以及其他类别的EAD申请,这反过来将全面缩短EAD申请的处理时间。一旦(c)(8)类EAD申请收件量下降,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)便可从容地将资源调配至庇护事务司,用于裁决支持职能(辅以适当的交叉培训)或文书和行政职能,而在当前为维持Rosario案合规所必需的运营状态下,这两项调配此前根本无法实现。
As detailed above, DHS is primarily attempting to resolve the issues surrounding the asylum backlog but is having difficulty even reaching those ( printed page 8651) cases due to many operational concerns and competing adjudications priorities. DHS can circumstantially establish that these proposed reforms would help DHS achieve its stated goals—which are the same as the INS's goals in 1994. For purposes of a hypothetical, assuming DHS publishes a final rule aligned with the proposed rule here and achieves similar results to what the INS achieved in 1994—a nearly 80 percent reduction in asylum applications in only five FYs—DHS could then reallocate asylum resources and more successfully tackle the looming backlog. DHS could also move its EAD adjudicatory resources to support timely adjudication of initial (c)(8) EAD applications as well as other EAD application categories, which in turn reduces processing times for EAD applications across the board. Once (c)(8) EAD receipts decrease, USCIS could comfortably surge resources to the Asylum Division for adjudications support functions (with appropriate cross training) or clerical and administrative functions, both of which have simply not been possible with the current state of operations necessary to maintain Rosario compliance.
例如,在FY2024(可获得完整数据的最近一个完整财政年度),DHS共收到422,457件庇护申请。[214] 暂且假设DHS能够借助本规则复制INS当年的成果,实现庇护申请减少80%,那么DHS的新庇护申请量将降至约84,491件(即422,457件的20%)。与此同时,按照FY2024的人员配置水平,DHS共批准17,175件、行政性结案107,007件、拒绝或移交5,709件,即针对待决庇护申请作出的最终裁定和行政性结案总计129,891件。[215] 若其他变量保持不变,且本规则的影响达到与INS 1994年规则制定相似的效果,那么在FY2024的人员配置水平下,DHS的裁决量将达到预计新庇护申请收件量的153%。
For example, in FY2024, the last full year of data available, DHS received 422,457 asylum applications.[214] Assuming for a moment DHS can replicate INS's results with this rule, achieving an 80% reduction in asylum filings, then DHS new asylum filings would drop closer to 84,491 (20% of 422,457). At the same time, using staffing levels from FY2024, DHS approved 17,175, administratively closed 107,007, and denied or referred 5,709—for a total of 129,891 final decisions and administrative closures on pending asylum applications.[215] If all other variables remained constant and the impacts of this rulemaking yielded a similar result as the INS's 1994 rulemaking, then at FY2024 staffing levels DHS would be adjudicating 153% of the projected new asylum filing receipt volumes.
再以FY2025数据(截至2025年5月22日)为例,可以得出更佳的结果。DHS本年度(年初至今)共收到331,883件庇护申请。[216] 同样,暂且假设DHS能够借助本现行规则复制INS以往的成果,实现庇护申请减少80%,那么DHS的新庇护申请量将降至约66,376件(即331,883件的20%)。按照当前的人员配置水平,在FY2025,DHS共批准8,667件、行政性结案159,530件、拒绝或移交11,872件,即针对待决庇护申请作出的最终裁定总计180,069件。[217] 从FY2025的部分数据来看,若其他变量保持不变,且本规则制定的影响达到与INS 1994年规则制定相似的效果,那么按照当前人员配置水平,DHS的裁决量将达到预计新庇护申请收件量的240%。
Looking at FY2025 data (through May 22, 2025) as another example, an even better result is reached. DHS received 331,883 asylum applications this year (YTD).[216] Again, assuming for a moment DHS can replicate INS's past results with this current rule, achieving an 80% reduction in asylum filings, then DHS new asylum filings would drop closer to 66,376 (20% of 331,883). At current staffing levels, in FY2025 DHS approved 8,667, administratively closed 159,530, and denied or referred 11,872—for a total of 180,069 final decisions on pending asylum applications.[217] Looking at partial FY2025 data, if all other variables remained constant and the impacts of this rulemaking yielded a similar result as the INS's 1994 rulemaking, then at current staffing DHS would be adjudicating 240% of the projected new asylum filing receipt volumes.
DHS指出,在此假设情形下,某些变量不会保持不变。值得注意的是,随着新庇护申请收件量和庇护积压案件数量的下降,初次和续签(c)(8)类EAD申请量也将自然而然地随之下降。由于拟议的法规变更导致庇护申请收件量减少,庇护官员将能够投入更多时间处理USCIS的庇护申请积压案件。与此同时,(c)(8)类EAD申请量的减少将使USCIS能够更高效地将EAD裁决人员调配至其他EAD申请类别,从而力求全面缩短处理时间。DHS还指出,当年FY1995的新庇护申请峰值(149,566件)已在最近四个财政年度中全部被超越(FY2022为247,790件;FY2023为464,398件;FY2024为422,457件;FY2025为331,883件(截至2025年5月22日)),[218]因此,尽管本假设情形中的各项假设具备可行性,但新庇护申请的庞大体量可能会减缓DHS达到INS当年成果的速度(例如,可能需要8年或10年,而非5年)。然而,DHS有信心,如果这些拟议变更最终定稿,DHS将取得与INS在1994年法规改革后相似的成果。
DHS notes that certain variables would not remain constant with this hypothetical. Notably, as both new asylum filing receipt and asylum backlog volumes decline, initial and renewal (c)(8) EADs filings organically would decline as well. As asylum filing receipts decrease as a result of the proposed regulatory changes, asylum officer resources will be able to devote more time to the USCIS asylum application backlog. At the same time, the reduction in (c)(8) EAD filings will allow USCIS to more efficiently allocate EAD adjudications staff across other EAD filing categories in an effort to reduce overall processing times across the board. DHS also notes that the then-peak of new asylum filings in FY1995 (149,566) has been surpassed in all four of the last FYs (FY2022 247,790; FY2023 464,398; FY2024 422,457; and FY2025 331,883 (through May 22, 2025)),[218] so while the assumptions in this hypothetical are feasible, the sheer volume of new asylum filings may slow the rate at which the INS's results are reached by DHS ( e.g., it may take 8 or 10 years instead of 5). However, DHS is confident that if these proposed changes are finalized, DHS will achieve a result similar to the INS after its 1994 regulatory reforms.
基于支持本规则的数据及本文所述理由,国土安全部(DHS)提议在法规中明文规定:当庇护申请人正面庇护申请的审理处理时间连续90天超过180天时,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)将暂停受理庇护申请人提交的初次(c)(8)类工作许可文件(EAD)申请,直至USCIS审理正面庇护申请的处理时间连续90天小于或等于180天为止。[219]在USCIS恢复受理庇护申请人提交的初次工作许可申请后,如果正面庇护申请审理的平均处理时间再次连续90天超过180天,USCIS将再次暂停受理(c)(8)类EAD申请。关于是否受理庇护申请人初次工作许可申请的决定,将由USCIS局长根据USCIS的处理时间作出,不涉及自由裁量权。该机构将在USCIS网站上公布何时受理及何时暂停受理初次(c)(8)类EAD申请。该公告还将同时公布支持USCIS局长所作决定的处理时间数据。国土安全部认为,更新网站是将其运作状态通知公众的最迅速、最便捷的方式。国土安全部还认为,此程序的关键环节之一在于提供作为USCIS局长决定依据的处理时间数据。因此,国土安全部提议按季度公布处理时间数据。需要特别指出的是,对庇护申请人初次工作许可申请的任何暂停,均不适用于续签(c)(8)类EAD申请;即使正面庇护申请审理的处理时间连续90天超过180天,USCIS仍将继续受理和审理续签申请。国土安全部决定采用90天的评估周期,因为该周期与国土安全部专家目前按季度汇编处理时间及其他统计数据的做法相吻合。
Based on the data supporting this rule and the justification described here, DHS proposes to codify in regulation that it will pause acceptance of initial (c)(8) EAD applications from asylum applicants when the processing times of adjudications of affirmative asylum applications exceeds 180 days for a period of 90 consecutive days, until the USCIS processing time for adjudicating affirmative asylum applications is less than or equal to 180 days for a period of 90 consecutive days.[219] After USCIS has resumed accepting initial employment authorization applications from asylum applicants, if the average processing times of adjudications of affirmative asylum applications again exceeds 180 days for a period of 90 consecutive days, USCIS would again pause the acceptance of (c)(8) EAD applications. The determinations as to whether initial employment authorization applications for asylum applicants are accepted or not would be made by the Director of USCIS, based on the USCIS processing times only and not subject to discretion. The agency would announce on the USCIS website when it will accept and when it pauses acceptance of initial (c)(8) EAD applications. The announcement would also be accompanied by the publication of the processing times, which support the determination made by the Director of USCIS. DHS believes that a website update is the most expeditious and accessible mode of notifying the public of its operational posture. DHS also believes that a critical part of this process will be to provide the processing times, which form the basis for the determinations made by the Director of USCIS. Therefore, DHS proposes to publish the quarterly processing times. It should be highlighted that any pause of initial employment authorization applications from asylum applicants would not apply to any renewal (c)(8) EAD applications, which would continue to be accepted and adjudicated by USCIS in the event the processing time of adjudications of affirmative asylum applications exceeds 180 days for a period of 90 consecutive days. DHS decided on a 90-day evaluation period, as it correlates with the current compilation of processing times and other statistics performed on a quarterly basis by DHS experts.
此外,由于这一变化以及本文件后文所述的365天等待期,今后将有更少的庇护申请人能够在其申请待决期间获得工作许可。只有当庇护申请的平均处理时间维持在180天或以下的情况下,庇护申请待决超过365天的外国人才有资格申请初次(c)(8)类工作许可证(EAD)。正如移民归化局（INS）在1994年所做的那样,美国国土安全部（DHS）已尽职地权衡了这一困难与维持一个正常运作的庇护制度之间的关系,以及遏制那些仅出于获取工作许可证之机会而提出的轻率、欺诈性或其他无实质理由的申请的必要性。DHS认为,目前庇护制度正因庇护申请数量过多而不堪重负,其中包括那些为获取工作许可而提出的轻率、欺诈性或其他(printed page 8652)无实质理由的庇护申请。DHS理解,庇护申请人可能是因种族、宗教、国籍、特定社会团体成员身份或政治观点而逃离过去的迫害,或对未来遭受迫害怀有充分根据的恐惧。《移民与国籍法》第208(b)(1)节,《美国法典》第8编第1158(b)(1)节。然而,该法规中并无任何规定要求为申请庇护的外国人提供工作许可证,这纯粹是一项自由裁量的EAD类别。庇护制度的宗旨在于对庇护申请作出及时回应。此后,其目标是为最终有资格获得庇护的外国人提供工作许可,而非向所有寻求庇护但最终可能不符合资格的外国人保证工作许可。通过暂停受理初次工作许可申请,该申请数量目前已超过每月15万件,[220]外国人将减少出于获取工作许可为目的而提出轻率、欺诈性或其他无实质理由庇护申请的动机,因此DHS预计庇护申请数量将随之下降,正如《非法移民改革和移民责任法》（IIRIRA）改革后数年间所出现的情况一样。随着轻率、欺诈性或其他无实质理由庇护申请的减少,美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）将有更大的空间,通过将更多可用的庇护官重新分配至积压案件,集中裁决力量处理现有的庇护积压案件,并致力于作出及时、公正的决定。(c)(8)类EAD申请数量的相应减少,也将使USCIS能够将EAD人员资源重新分配至其他EAD申请类别,从而全面缩短EAD处理时间。
In addition, as a result of this change and the 365-day waiting period described later in this document, moving forward, fewer asylum applicants will receive employment authorization while their applications are pending. Only aliens whose asylum applications are pending beyond 365 days while the average asylum application processing time remains at or below 180 days will be eligible to file for an initial (c)(8). EAD. Just as the INS did in 1994, DHS has dutifully balanced this hardship against the need for a functioning asylum system and the need to deter aliens filing frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless claims solely motivated by the opportunity to obtain an employment authorization document. DHS believes that the asylum system is currently over-burdened and overwhelmed by asylum applications, including the frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise by ( printed page 8652) meritless asylum applications filed by aliens who are seeking to obtain employment authorization. DHS understands that asylum applicants may be fleeing past persecution or may have a well-founded fear of future persecution on account of race, religion, nationality, membership in a particular social group, or political opinion. INA sec. 208(b)(1), 8 U.S.C. 1158(b)(1). However, there is nothing in the statute that requires an EAD for aliens applying for asylum, rather this is a purely discretionary EAD category. The intention behind the asylum system is to provide a timely response to an asylum claim. Thereafter, the aim is to provide employment authorizations to aliens ultimately eligible for asylum, not guarantee employment authorization to all aliens who seek asylum, but may ultimately not be eligible. By pausing the acceptance of initial employment authorization applications, which has now exceeded 150,000 applications per month,[220] aliens will have less incentive to file frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications for the purposes of obtaining employment authorization, and DHS expects that asylum filings will therefore decrease, as they did in the years following IIRIRA reform. With a decline in frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications USCIS would have greater bandwidth to focus adjudicative efforts on the existing asylum backlog by reallocating more available asylum officers to backlog cases and work toward providing timely and fair decisions. The accompanying decline in (c)(8) EAD applications would also allow USCIS to reallocate EAD staffing resources to other EAD application categories and decrease EAD processing times across the board.
USCIS认识到,由于USCIS正在努力裁决目前已经积压的大量庇护待决案件,对新的初次(c)(8)类EAD申请暂停受理的期限可能会较长。然而,USCIS认为这一暂停将导致新庇护申请量的下降幅度与1994年移民归化局（INS）制定规则后所出现的80%降幅相当。USCIS指出,该规则制定后获得庇护的外国人绝对数量基本保持稳定,这表明获得工作许可渠道的变化并未阻止具有正当理由的庇护申请人提出申请。USCIS认识到,在本拟议规则中,EAD申请受理的暂停期可能会大大长于1994年法规所实施的180天等待期,因此可能会有一些具有潜在正当理由的申请人因此被劝退而未提出申请。结合拟议的监管变化,USCIS打算总体上对庇护裁决继续维持后进先出（LIFO）处理方式,并相信申请接收量的大幅下降与近年来庇护官人数的显著增加相结合,将使USCIS能够消化积压案件,并在本规则生效后达到在180天时限内裁决新庇护申请的状态。
USCIS recognizes that the initial pause on acceptance of new initial (c)(8) EAD applications may be lengthy as USCIS works to adjudicate the substantial backlog of pending asylum cases that are already pending before USCIS. However, USCIS believes this pause will result in a decrease in new asylum receipts comparable to the 80% decrease that was seen as a result of the INS's 1994 rulemaking. USCIS notes that the absolute number of aliens granted asylum remained relatively consistent following that rulemaking, indicating that changes in access to employment authorization did not deter aliens with meritorious asylum applications from filing. USCIS recognizes that in this proposed rule, the pause of on EAD application acceptances will likely be significantly lengthier than the 180-day waiting period implemented through the 1994 regulation and therefore there may be some aliens with potentially meritorious filings who are deterred from filing. In conjunction with the proposed regulatory changes, USCIS intends to generally maintain its LIFO processing to asylum adjudications and believes that the combination of vastly decreased receipts and the significant increase in asylum officers over recent years will allow USCIS to work through the backlog and get to a place where the agency is adjudicating new asylum applications within the 180-day time period after this rule takes effect.
USCIS指出,这代表着恢复《移民与国籍法》(INA)这些条款及相关法规的既定运行方式。根据法规及现行法规的规定,因待决庇护申请而获得工作许可应属例外和不寻常的情形。通过将新申请(c)(8)类工作许可证(EAD)的资格与待处理的主动申请庇护案件积压量相挂钩,USCIS旨在确保INA的这一条款按照其既定目的运作,并消除那些提交无意义、欺诈性或无实质理由的庇护申请的外国人借此形成恶性循环的可能性,即通过压垮庇护系统并从中牟利,从而损害有正当理由的庇护申请人及美国民众的利益。
USCIS notes this represents a return to the intended functioning of these sections of the INA and regulations. Employment authorization due to a pending asylum application is intended by the statute and existing regulation to be exceptional and unusual. By linking the ability to receive a new applications for (c)(8) EAD to the pending affirmative asylum caseload, USCIS intends to ensure that this section of the INA functions as it was intended to and eliminates the ability for aliens filing frivolous, fraudulent, or meritless asylum applications to create a vicious cycle by overwhelming the asylum system and then profiting from doing so at the expense of meritorious asylum applicants and the American people.
B. 申请(c)(8)类工作许可证(EAD)的365个日历日等待期
B. 365 Calendar-Day Waiting Period To Apply for (c)(8) EADs
如前所述,导致庇护案件积压从而引发滥用庇护系统以获取工作许可的因素有很多。其中之一是近期对延缓执行遣返(deferred action)、假释(parole)及临时保护身份(TPS)的广泛使用。USCIS的数据显示,在2020财年,仅有104名获得延缓执行遣返的外国人随后提交了I-589表格,而到2025财年(年初至今)这一数字上升至1,158人,增幅为1,013%。[221]在2020财年,USCIS的数据显示,仅有758名获得假释的外国人随后提交了I-589表格,而到2025财年(年初至今)这一数字上升至156,242人,增幅为20,512%。[222]在2020财年,USCIS的数据显示,仅有66名获得临时保护身份的外国人随后提交了I-589表格,而到2025财年(年初至今)这一数字上升至43,512人,增幅为65,827%。[223]显然,这些项目本不应旨在为外国人提供永久性的移民身份。对这些项目的广泛使用不仅进一步加重了本已不堪重负的庇护系统的负担,还增加了非法外国人以及其他仅具有临时身份且获得美国永久身份可能性较低的外国人的存在。提交庇护申请正是处于此种境地的外国人寻求留在美国的一种方式。
As discussed previously, there are many factors that have contributed to the backlog of asylum cases that leads to the abuse of the asylum system for employment authorization. Among those has been the recent expansive use of deferred action, parole, and temporary protected status (TPS). In FY 2020, USCIS data show only 104 aliens with deferred action who subsequently filed a Form I-589; by FY 2025 (YTD) that number rose to 1,158—a 1,013% increase.[221] In FY 2020, USCIS data show only 758 aliens with parole who subsequently filed a Form I-589; by FY 2025 (YTD) that number rose to 156,242—a 20,512% increase.[222] In FY 2020, USCIS data show only 66 aliens with TPS who subsequently filed a Form I-589; by FY 2025 (YTD) that number rose to 43,512—a 65,827% increase.[223] These programs, quite simply, were not intended to provide permanent immigration status to aliens. The expansive use of these programs has not only further taxed the already strained asylum system, but also increased the presence of illegal aliens and other aliens with only temporary status and low likelihood of obtaining permanent status in the United States. Filing an application for asylum is one such way an alien in this position may seek to remain in the United States.
在过去数十年间,美国国土安全部(DHS)一直行使自由裁量权,广泛地批准假释、授予延缓执行遣返或行使临时假释权限,从而形成了各类分类性假释项目。[224]《移民与国籍法》赋予部长仅限于个案情形下,因紧急人道主义原因或重大公共利益,方可依[DHS]规定的条件,临时批准外国人入境申请假释的狭义自由裁量权。[225]尽管这些假释项目(见原文第8653页)已经终止,但许多外国人仍滞留在美国,且往往没有获得合法居留身份的途径。近年来,假释批准数量极为庞大,2022财年共批准假释795,561人次,2023财年则达到1,340,002人次。[226]
Over the span of decades, DHS has exercised discretionary authority to parole, grant deferred action, or exercise temporary parole authority expansively to create categorical parole programs.[224] The INA confers upon the Secretary the narrow discretionary authority to parole applicants for admission into the United States “temporarily under such conditions as [DHS] may prescribe only on a case-by-case basis for urgent humanitarian reasons or significant public benefit.” [225] While those parole ( printed page 8653) programs were terminated, many aliens are still in the United States, often without a pathway to lawful residence in the United States. Parole grants in recent years have been extremely large, with 795,561 parole grants in FY 2022 and 1,340,002 parole grants in FY 2023.[226]
此外,延缓执行遣返的使用也大幅扩展。延缓执行遣返是DHS行使自由裁量权的一种形式,即DHS选择不对某外国人寻求遣返,尽管该外国人缺乏合法身份或在其他方面属于可遣返对象。与假释不同,延缓执行遣返并非由法律设立,《移民与国籍法》中也未对其作出明确界定。不采取执法行动的决定属于该机构的自由裁量范围。[227]延缓执行遣返从未意图取代现行合法移民程序,或仅为使不可准入、可遣返或在其他方面不符合资格的外国人得以留在美国,直至其有资格获得合法身份而提供长期救济。[228]规模最大的分类性延缓执行遣返项目是“童年入境者暂缓遣返计划”(DACA),截至2024年9月,约有538,000[229]名外国人凭借DACA身份居住在美国。[230]
In addition, the use of deferred action has expanded significantly. Deferred action is a form of discretion in which DHS chooses to not seek an alien's removal from the United States, though the alien lacks lawful status or is otherwise removable from the United States. Unlike parole, deferred action was not created by statute and is not specifically defined in the INA. The decision not to take an enforcement action is within the discretion of the agency.[227] Deferred action was never meant to supplant the current legal immigration process or provide long-term relief solely to allow an inadmissible, removable, or otherwise ineligible alien to remain in the United States until he or she can qualify for a legal status.[228] The largest categorical deferred action program is Deferred Action for Childhood Arrivals (DACA), and as of September 2024 approximately 538,000 [229] aliens were living in the United States with DACA.[230]
临时保护身份(TPS)是又一项不导致美国长期合法身份的项目。根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第244条,即8 U.S.C. 1254a,国土安全部部长在与美国政府相关机构协商并确定该外国(或其部分地区)存在特定条件后,可将该外国(或其部分地区)指定为临时保护身份国,此类条件包括持续武装冲突等将对返回该国的外国人安全构成严重威胁的情形。[231]此后,部长可向该外国的合格国民或曾惯常居住于该国但无国籍的合格外国人授予临时保护身份。[232]此外,国土安全部(DHS)有时会对已获临时保护身份指定的国家重新作出指定,并允许在该国临时保护身份初始指定之后进入美国的外国人新近获得临时保护身份资格。[233]2024日历年度,临时保护身份受益人约为1,396,586人。[234]
TPS is yet another program that does not lead to long-term legal status in the United States. Under section 244 of the Immigration and Nationality Act (INA), 8 U.S.C. 1254a, the Secretary of Homeland Security may designate a foreign state (or part thereof) for TPS after consulting with appropriate agencies of the U.S. Government and determining that there are specified conditions present in that foreign state or part of a foreign state, such as ongoing armed conflict that would pose a serious threat to the safety of retuning aliens.[231] The Secretary may then grant TPS to eligible nationals of that foreign state or eligible aliens having no nationality who last habitually resided in that state.[232] In addition, DHS has at times re-designated countries for TPS and allowed aliens who entered the United States after the initial designation of TPS to be newly eligible for TPS.[233] In Calendar Year 2024 there were approximately 1,396,586 TPS beneficiaries.[234]
多年来对这三项项目的广泛使用,已造成大量非法外国人群体,这些人除寻求庇护外,没有其他途径获得在美国的永久居留身份。对这些项目的广泛使用进一步助长了外国人提出无实际根据、欺诈性或其他无理由庇护申请以获得工作许可的动机。[235]尽管这些项目各自都提供了获得工作许可的途径,但均为特定且有时间限制的期限。由于庞大的积压案件,尽管缺乏庇护依据,庇护仍然是外国人为获得长期工作许可而采取的具有吸引力的选择。
The expansive use of these three programs over the years has created a very large population of illegal aliens that do not have pathways to permanent residence in the United States outside of seeking asylum. The expansive use of these programs has further incentivized aliens to file frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications to gain employment authorization.[235] While each of these programs provides access for employment authorization, it is for a specific and time-limited period. Asylum remains an attractive option for aliens to secure employment authorization for an extended period of time, despite lacking a basis for asylum, due to the enormous backlog.
基于上述背景,国土安全部在本规则中拟将庇护申请人在根据待决庇护申请获得工作许可资格之前必须等待的时间,从180天延长至365个日历日。见8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)。国土安全部还拟取消获得就业authorization文件(EAD)资格的单独等待期,使外国人可以在同一时间申请并获得工作许可。目前,庇护申请人可在其庇护申请待决满150天后申请工作许可,并可在其庇护申请待决满180天后(不包括因外国人自身原因造成的任何延误)获得EAD。见8 CFR 208.7(a)(1)。在现行模式下,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)和外国人均须追踪两个时间框架:150天等待期和180天庇护EAD时钟。该时钟的计算会因延误而启动或停止,具体取决于该延误是机构造成的延误还是外国人造成的延误。见8 CFR 208.7(a)(2)。如本序言D.3.a节所述,该制度对外国人和USCIS而言都过于复杂且负担过重。因此,国土安全部拟在法规中将申请等待期和资格等待期合并为一个简单明了的时间线,即365个日历日。[236]
With this background, DHS is proposing in this rule to extend the time period an asylum applicant must wait before he or she is eligible to be granted employment authorization based on a pending asylum application from 180 days to 365 calendar days. See8 CFR 208.7(a)(1). DHS also proposes to eliminate the separate waiting periods for eligibility to receive an EAD, so that aliens are eligible to apply and be granted employment authorization at the same time. Currently, an asylum applicant may file for employment authorization once their application for asylum has been pending for 150 days and may receive an EAD after their application for asylum has been pending for 180 days, excluding any alien-caused delays. 8 CFR 208.7(a)(1). Under the current model, both USCIS and the alien must track two timeframes: the 150-day waiting period, and the 180-day Asylum EAD Clock. The clock calculation is subject to starts and stops based on delays, depending on whether the delay is an agency-caused delay or an alien-caused delay. 8 CFR 208.7(a)(2). As described in section D.3.a of this preamble, this system is complicated and overly burdensome on both the alien and USCIS. Thus, DHS proposes to codify in regulation that it will merge the waiting period to apply and the waiting period to be eligible into one, straight-forward timeline: 365 calendar days.[236]
根据拟议规则,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)将不再需要在计算180天庇护就业许可文件(EAD)时钟时考虑外国人造成的延误,而是将简单地从庇护申请收到之日起计算365个日历日,以确定外国人何时可以申请就业授权。移民归化局(INS)此前选择180天等待期,是为了阻止那些提出无实质理由的庇护申请人提交轻率、欺诈性或无实质理由的申请以获取就业授权。59 FR 62284(1994年12月5日)。由于180天等待期不再起到威慑作用,国土安全部(DHS)提议将该时间段以法规形式修改为365天等待期。(第8654页打印版)美国公民及移民服务局指出,现行法规允许申请人在第150天提交申请,并内置30天的处理时间缓冲期,从而累计达到180天;但美国公民及移民服务局现提议取消该处理时间缓冲期,使外国人在其庇护申请收到之日起365天之前不得提出申请。再加上180天的裁定时限,这些变化可能使就业授权文件的总等待时间增加至545天。通过选择一个超过庇护申请180天目标处理时间的等待期,国土安全部希望借此阻止轻率、欺诈性或无实质理由的申请,进而能够更快地批准具有实质理由的庇护申请,确保只有获批庇护申请的人员才能在365个日历日内工作。
Under the proposed rule, USCIS will no longer have to account for alien-caused delays in calculating the 180-day Asylum EAD Clock, but will instead simply calculate 365 calendar days from the asylum application receipt date to determine when an alien can request employment authorization. The INS previously chose the 180-day waiting period to deter aliens who are meritless asylum seekers from filing frivolous, fraudulent, or meritless claims to obtain employment authorization. 59 FR 62284 (Dec. 5, 1994). As the 180-day waiting period is no longer providing a deterrence, DHS proposes to codify in regulation that it will change the time ( printed page 8654) period to a 365-day waiting period. USCIS notes that the current regulations allow the applicant to submit the application at 150 days and then builds in the 30-day processing timeframe buffer to add up to 180 days; but USCIS proposes now to eliminate the processing time buffer, so that that aliens may not apply until 365 days after their asylum application is received. Coupled with the 180-day adjudication timeframe, these changes could increase the total waiting period for an EAD to 545 days.
