Settlement Agreement, USCIS will not rely on any determination by DOJ that an alien is not a UAC.[288] Rather, USCIS exercises initial jurisdiction over the adjudication of the UAC's asylum application and renders its own jurisdictional ( printed page 8663) determination.[289] Accordingly, UACs who have been denied asylum by an IJ, the BIA, or a Federal court may still have a pending asylum application before USCIS because USCIS retains initial jurisdiction over asylum applications filed by UACs.[290] Therefore, if a UAC's asylum application remains pending before USCIS, which is the basis for the alien's (c)(8) EAD, his or her (c)(8) EAD will not automatically terminate even if his or her asylum application is denied by an IJ, BIA, or a Federal court.
J. 因I-765表格裁决中存在不利信息而优先裁决庇护申请
J. Prioritizing the Adjudication of an Asylum Application Due to Derogatory Information in the Form I-765 Adjudication
为进一步遏制仅以获取工作授权为唯一目的而提出的无实质依据、欺诈性或其他无理的庇护申请，美国国土安全部（DHS）提议在法规中明确规定如下权力：在任何（c）(8)类工作许可文件（EAD）裁定过程中发现不利信息的庇护申请，可优先安排裁定。例如，若美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）在对（c）(8)类工作授权申请人进行安全审查时发现其存在国家安全风险，USCIS可标记相应的庇护申请，以便庇护案件审查官能够更迅速地安排面谈并作出裁定。在对续签（c）(8)类申请人进行安全审查时，USCIS可能发现新的定罪或逮捕记录，从而需要优先安排庇护面谈或裁定。例如，某庇护申请人在提出庇护申请并获得初次工作许可文件时可能并无逮捕记录，但此后可能已被判定犯有加重重罪。在此情况下，工作许可文件裁定人员可将该案标记给对该申请拥有管辖权的庇护办公室，以便优先安排面谈和裁定。上述两个示例的结果均在于更迅速地裁定存在风险的申请人案件，并减少此类案件在积压案件中滞留的时间。
In furtherance of the effort to deter frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum filings for the sole purpose of obtaining employment authorization, DHS proposes to codify in regulation the authority to prioritize for adjudication an asylum application in which derogatory information is encountered during any (c)(8) EAD adjudications. For example, if USCIS discovers a national security risk while conducting security checks on a (c)(8) employment authorization applicant, USCIS may flag the corresponding asylum application so that an asylum adjudicator may more rapidly schedule the case for an interview and make a decision on the case. In conducting security checks on renewal (c)(8) applicants, USCIS may discover new convictions or arrests that warrant a prioritized asylum interview or adjudication. For example, an asylum applicant may have had no arrest record at the time he or she applied for asylum and received the initial EAD, but he or she may have since been convicted of an aggravated felony. In that instance, an EAD adjudicator could flag the case for the asylum office with jurisdiction over the application, so that the interview and adjudication could be prioritized. The outcome in both of these examples is to more rapidly adjudicate cases where applicants present risks and to reduce the time cases such as these linger in the backlog.
这一重大变化将使本部门能够迅速识别并高效地将对美国构成国家安全或公共安全威胁的不合格外国人驱逐出境，同时减少排队待审案件的数量。
This significant change would allow the Department to quickly identify and efficiently remove ineligible aliens who pose a national security or public safety threat to the United States, while simultaneously decreasing the pending applications in queue.
此外，正如本拟议规则制定通知（NPRM）通篇所述，积压的正向庇护申请案件量已成为美国国土安全部（DHS）的巨大负担，截至2024财年末已增长至超过145万件。[291] 伴随积压的正向庇护申请案件量不断增长的，是正向及抗辩性庇护申请人提交的（c）(8)类工作许可文件申请数量。基于待决庇护申请而提出的初次工作许可文件申请逐年稳步增加，USCIS在2014财年收到62,169份（c）(8)类工作许可文件申请，2017财年收到261,793份此类申请，随后在2023财年出现爆炸性增长，达到802,753份（c）(8)类工作许可文件申请。[292] 2024财年，USCIS共收到1,200,533份初次（c）(8)类工作许可文件申请。[293] 为遏制无实质依据、欺诈性或其他无理的庇护申请，DHS于2018年恢复采用后进先出（LIFO）面谈排期方式，使DHS能够迅速将近期提交的无理庇护申请移交处理，并将相关外国人置于驱逐程序之中。同样地，本拟议条款将允许DHS在发现不利信息的案件中优先完成对无理庇护申请的裁定，从而使本部门避免进一步加剧呈指数级增长的庇护积压案件。这一变化可能导致无不利信息的外国人的处理时间略有延长，但本部门认为任何额外延迟都是微乎其微的，并指出这不应成为首要关切，尤其是因为这些外国人仍将保有申请（c）(8)类工作许可文件的资格。相反，DHS选择优先考虑国家安全与公共安全，并优先减少因提交无实质依据、欺诈性或其他无理庇护申请的外国人，以及根本不符合庇护资格的外国人所造成的积压案件。
Additionally, as referenced throughout this NPRM, the caseload of pending affirmative asylum applications has become an enormous burden on DHS and has grown to more than 1.45 million as of the end of FY 2024.[291] Alongside the growing pending affirmative asylum application caseload is the accompanying number of (c)(8) EAD applications filed by affirmative and defensive asylum applicants. Initial applications for EADs based on pending asylum applications have steadily increased over the years, with USCIS receiving 62,169 (c)(8) EAD applications in FY 2014, 261,793 (c)(8) EAD applications in FY 2017, and then seeing an explosive jump to 802,753 (c)(8) EAD applications in FY 2023.[292] In FY 2024, USCIS received 1,200,533 initial (c)(8) EAD applications.[293] In order to deter frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum claims, in 2018 DHS returned to a LIFO interview scheduling approach, where DHS could refer recently filed meritless asylum applications quickly and place those aliens into removal proceedings. Similarly, this proposed provision will allow DHS to prioritize the completion of meritless asylum applications in cases where derogatory information is identified, allowing the Department to avoid adding to the exponentially increasing asylum backlog. This change could lead to slightly longer processing times for aliens without derogatory information, but the Department believes any additional time would be de minimis and notes that should not be the paramount concern, especially since those aliens will still remain eligible to apply for (c)(8) EADs. Rather, DHS is choosing to prioritize national security and public safety and the reduction of backlogged cases for aliens who filed frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications, as well as aliens who are simply ineligible for asylum.
K. 对应的美国司法部（DOJ）法规
K. Corresponding DOJ Regulations
2003年，作为反映美国国土安全部（DHS）设立及DOJ与新设DHS之间职能转移的法规修订的一部分，8 CFR 208.3节和208.7节被复制至新设的8 CFR第五章中。参见68 FR 9824, 9834（2003年2月28日）。当时，DOJ将整个第208部分的内容复制至为移民法院行政办公室（EOIR）新设的第1208部分，原因在于这些条款“彼此关联紧密，难以进行简单的管辖划分”，并表示DOJ预计两部门将在日后开展进一步的规则制定，以完善职权划分。同上，第9826页。
In 2003, 8 CFR 208.3 and 208.7 were duplicated in a new 8 CFR Chapter V as part of the amendments to the regulations to reflect the creation of the Department of Homeland Security and the transfer of functions between DOJ and the new DHS. See68 FR 9824, 9834 (Feb. 28, 2003). At the time, DOJ duplicated the entire Part 208 into the new Part 1208 for EOIR because the provisions were “so interrelated that no simple division of jurisdiction is possible” and stated that DOJ expected the Departments to engage in further rulemaking to refine the division of authorities at a later date. Id. 9826.
2020年,美国移民上诉审查执行办公室(EOIR)修订了8 CFR 1208.3(c)(3)中有关庇护申请表格的EOIR条文,包括删除了关于申请工作许可需等待150天的规定,并将8 CFR 1208.7中有关庇护申请人工作许可文件的EOIR条文予以保留(reserved)。85 FR 81698(2020年12月16日)。然而,正如本序言第III.D.3.a节所述,该规则已被初步禁止执行。Nat'l Immigrant Justice Ctr., Np. 21-56 (RBW)。因此,现行有效版本的8 CFR 1208.7不再正式处于“保留”状态。尽管如此,1208.7以及1208.3(c)(3)中关于申请工作许可需等待150天的规定并无实质效力,因为美国司法部(DOJ)无权裁决工作许可申请。参见85 FR 59692, 59696(2020年9月23日)(解释司法部决定删除庇护申请人可申请工作许可的具体等待期限的决定,“以确保EOIR法规不与DHS有关工作许可资格的法规相抵触”,并保留8 CFR 1208.7,原因是“EOIR不裁决工作许可申请”)。
In 2020, EOIR amended the EOIR provision at 8 CFR 1208.3(c)(3) regarding the form of an asylum application, including by removing the reference to a 150-day waiting period for filing for employment authorization, and reserved the EOIR provision at 8 CFR 1208.7 regarding employment authorization documents for asylum applicants. 85 FR 81698 (Dec. 16, 2020). However, as noted in section III.D.3.a of this preamble, that rule was preliminarily enjoined. Nat'l Immigrant Justice Ctr., Np. 21-56 (RBW). Accordingly, the currently effective version of 8 CFR 1208.7 is no longer officially reserved. Nonetheless, 1208.7 and the reference to a 150-day wait for filing for employment authorization at 1208.3(c)(3) do not have substantive effect because DOJ has no authority to adjudicate employment authorization applications. Cf.85 FR 59692, 59696 (Sep. 23, 2020) (explaining DOJ's decision to remove the specific time period after which asylum applicants may file an application for employment authorization “to ensure that EOIR regulations do not contradict DHS regulations regarding employment authorization eligibility,” and to reserve 8 CFR 1208.7 because “EOIR does not adjudicate applications for employment authorization.”).
因此,美国国土安全部(DHS)认识到,本规则将导致DHS在8 CFR 208.3和8 CFR 208.7中的法规与DOJ在8 CFR 1208.3和8 CFR 1208.7中的法规之间出现不一致。尽管如此,自本最终规则生效之日起,8 CFR 208.3和8 CFR 208.7的修订文本将适用于DHS及其裁决工作。DHS一直就本规则制定事宜与DOJ进行磋商,DOJ可能在日后发布相应的一致性修订。(印刷页码第8664页)
Accordingly, DHS recognizes that this rule would result in inconsistencies between the DHS regulations at 8 CFR 208.3 and 8 CFR 208.7 and the DOJ regulations at 8 CFR 1208.3 and 8 CFR 1208.7. Nevertheless, as of the effective date of this final rule, the revised language of 8 CFR 208.3 and 8 CFR 208.7 would govern DHS and its adjudications. DHS has been in consultation with DOJ on this rulemaking, and DOJ may issue conforming changes at a later date. ( printed page 8664)
六、法定和法规要求
VI. Statutory and Regulatory Requirements
A. 第12866号行政命令(《监管规划与审查》)、第13563号行政命令(《改进监管和监管审查》)以及第14192号行政命令(《通过放松监管释放繁荣》)
A. Executive Orders 12866 (Regulatory Planning and Review), 13563 (Improving Regulation and Regulatory Review), and 14192 (Unleashing Prosperity Through Deregulation)
第12866号行政命令(《监管规划与审查》)和第13563号行政命令(《改进监管和监管审查》)指示各机构评估现有监管方案的成本和效益,并在确有必要进行监管的情况下,选择能使净效益最大化的监管方式。第13563号行政命令强调量化成本和效益、降低成本、协调法规以及促进灵活性的重要性。第14192号行政命令(《通过放松监管释放繁荣》)指示各机构大幅减少遵守联邦法规所需的私人支出,并规定“在法律允许的范围内,与新法规相关的任何新增增量成本,应通过取消至少10项现有法规所产生的现有成本来予以抵消”。
E.O. 12866 (Regulatory Planning and Review) and 13563 (Improving Regulation and Regulatory Review) direct agencies to assess the costs and benefits of available regulatory alternatives and, if regulation is necessary, to select regulatory approaches that maximize net benefits. Executive Order 13563 emphasizes the importance of quantifying costs and benefits, reducing costs, harmonizing rules, and promoting flexibility. Executive Order 14192 (Unleashing Prosperity Through Deregulation) directs agencies to significantly reduce the private expenditures required to comply with Federal regulations and provides that “any new incremental costs associated with the new regulations shall, to the extent permitted by law be offset by the elimination of existing costs associated with at least 10 prior regulations.”
管理和预算办公室(OMB)已将本规则认定为根据第12866号行政命令第3(f)(1)节所定义的具有经济意义的“重大监管行动”。因此,本规则已经OMB审查。
The Office of Management and Budget (OMB) has designated this rule a “significant regulatory action” that is economically significant as defined under section 3(f)(1) of E.O. 12866. Accordingly, the rule has been reviewed by OMB.
此外,本规则不属于第14192号行政命令(见第5(a)节)所指的监管行动,因为本规则是针对美国移民相关职能发布的。本规则的主要直接目的是实施或解释美国移民法律(如《移民与国籍法》(INA)第101(a)(17)节,8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(17)所述),或美国联邦政府就外国人履行的任何其他职能。参见OMB备忘录M-25-20,《关于实施题为“通过放松监管释放繁荣”的第14192号行政命令第3节的指南》(2025年3月26日)。
Additionally, this rule is not an Executive Order 14192 (see 5(a)) regulatory action because it is being issued with respect to an immigration-related function of the United States. The rule's primary direct purpose is to implement or interpret the immigration laws of the United States (as described in INA sec. 101(a)(17), 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(17)) or any other function performed by the U.S. Federal Government with respect to aliens. See OMB Memorandum M-25-20, “Guidance Implementing Section 3 of Executive Order 14192, titled “Unleashing Prosperity Through Deregulation” (Mar. 26, 2025).
拟议规则将影响为有未决庇护申请的外国人签发工作许可文件((c)(8)类EAD)的行政程序、(c)(8)类EAD申请的处理时限、申请并获得(c)(8)类EAD的等待期、(c)(8)类EAD的有效期,以及(c)(8)类EAD的资格要求。本规则将要求对现行法规文本进行修改,并制定新的法规文本。
The proposed rule would impact the administrative process for issuance of EADs for aliens with a pending asylum application ((c)(8) EAD), processing timeframe for (c)(8) EAD applications, waiting period to apply for and receive a (c)(8) EAD, (c)(8) EAD validity period, and eligibility requirements for (c)(8) EADs. The rule will require changes to existing regulatory text and the creation of new regulatory text.
1. 拟议条款摘要及效益与成本影响
1. Summary of Proposed Provisions and Benefits and Costs Impacts
美国国土安全部（DHS）预计这项拟议规则将产生重大效益。庇护系统不堪重负，联邦裁决资源捉襟见肘，积案中的正面庇护申请如同磁石一般将外国人非法吸引至美国。过去几年庇护申请和相关就业授权文件（EAD）申请数量的激增，已经造成了难以维系的局面。本拟议规则将使美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）受益，使其能够按照长期可持续的案件处理时限运作庇护申请人的初始EAD申请，从而有充分时间处理国家安全、公共安全或欺诈方面的问题，并保持在证件制作和身份核验方面的技术进步。正如下文所引用的1994年移民归化局（INS）规则制定一样，DHS预计此项行动将减少无理和欺诈性的庇护申请，以及基于无实质依据的庇护申请而获取EAD的不正当经济激励。59 FR 14779（1994年3月30日）；59 FR 62284（1994年12月5日）。无理的、欺诈性的和无实质依据的庇护申请及相关就业授权申请可能成为吸引非法移民的磁石，并对地方、州、国民经济造成成本负担，使资源更加紧张。DHS预计这些变化将减少外国人及公众对尚未决定的庇护申请人EAD要求的困惑，有助于确保法规文本反映DHS当前政策，更忠实地落实法律的立法意图，同时提升项目的廉正性。DHS目前无法量化本拟议规则的全部潜在效益。
DHS expects that this proposed rule will generate substantial benefits. The asylum system is overwhelmed, federal adjudications resources are strained, and the affirmative asylum application backlog serves as a magnet pulling aliens into the U.S. illegally. The surge in both asylum filings and associated EADs over the past few years has created an untenable situation. This proposed rule would benefit USCIS by allowing it to operate under long-term, sustainable case processing times for initial EAD applications for asylum applicants, to allow sufficient time to address national security, public safety, or fraud concerns, and to maintain technological advances in document production and identity verification. Just as the 1994 INS rulemaking referenced below, DHS expects that this action will reduce frivolous and fraudulent asylum claims and perverse economic incentives to obtain an EAD under meritless asylum claims. 59 FR 14779 (Mar. 30, 1994); 59 FR 62284 (Dec. 5, 1994). Frivolous, fraudulent, and meritless asylum applications and related filings for employment authorization can serve as a magnet for illegal immigration and generate costs to localities, states, the national economy, and strain resources. DHS expects that these changes would reduce confusion regarding EAD requirements for aliens with pending asylum applications and the public, help ensure the regulatory text reflects current DHS policy and more faithfully implements the intent of the statute while simultaneously improving program integrity. DHS cannot currently quantify all of the potential benefits of this proposed rule.
此外，如果雇主能够雇用美国工人来填补原本由庇护申请人担任的职位，则此类外国人收入的变化将转化为有利于美国工人的工资和福利转移，并可能不会给雇主带来生产力损失或成本。尽管没有工作授权的外国人可能需要依靠其社会和支持网络（其中可能包括公共实体）的援助，但这可能存在一种抵消效应；由于本规则可能会减少移民人数，州、地方政府及非政府组织在目前向庇护申请人提供的公共援助和资源方面所承受的经济压力可能会减轻。此外，DHS预计本拟议规则将减少非法移民以及庇护申请和EAD方面的欺诈性申请。
In addition, if employers are able to hire American workers to fill the jobs the asylum applicants would hold, the change in earnings to such aliens would constitute beneficial wage and benefit transfers to American workers and would potentially pose no productivity loss or costs to employers. While it is possible that aliens without work authorization could require assistance from their social and support networks, which could include public entities, there could be a counterbalance; as this rule potentially will reduce immigration, there could be less of an economic strain on states, local government, and non-governmental organizations, in terms of any public assistance and resources that are currently provided to asylum applicants. Furthermore, DHS anticipates this proposed rule would decrease illegal migration and fraudulent claims for asylum applications and EADs.
本拟议规则带来的其他难以量化的效益包括：减少并预防潜在的欺诈案件、为寻求庇护的外国人提供一致性和明确性，以及简化USCIS初始(c)(8)类EAD申请流程，以实现可持续的案件处理时限。DHS无法估算这些潜在的间接影响（无论是成本、效益还是转移效应）或二阶及以上效应，因为这些超出了本分析的范围。本规则制定旨在减少无理的、欺诈性的和无实质依据的庇护申请及其相关的(c)(8)类EAD申请，同时改进USCIS为具有合理庇护申请依据的外国人签发就业授权文件的行政流程。
Additional, unquantifiable benefits resulting from this proposed rule include reduction and prevention of potential fraudulent cases, providing consistency and clarity to aliens seeking asylum, and streamlining the initial USCIS (c)(8) EAD request process for sustainable case processing times. DHS cannot estimate these potential indirect impacts (whether costs, benefits, transfers) or second order effects and beyond, as they are beyond the scope of this analysis. This rulemaking seeks to reduce frivolous, fraudulent, and meritless asylum applications and their associated applications for (c)(8) EADs while improving the administrative process for issuance of employment authorization documents for aliens with meritorious asylum application at USCIS.
要求外国人在提交初始和续签就业授权申请时均提交生物识别信息，将使DHS能够将该外国人的生物识别信息与政府数据库进行比对，以确定其是否与在案的任何犯罪活动相匹配，核实该外国人的身份，并便利证件的制作。此外，生物识别信息的采集使DHS能够确认个人未使用多重身份，或多人未共用同一身份。最后，通过生物识别信息的采集，DHS将通过确保只有持续寻求庇护的合格外国人才能申请并获得工作授权，从而提升项目的廉正性。这通常也会为公众带来益处，因为它提高了与申请及备案要求相关的透明度。正如序言部分所述，庇护项目历来一直面临身份欺诈方面的问题。
Requiring aliens to submit biometrics for both initial and renewal requests for employment authorization would enable DHS to vet an alien's biometrics against government databases to determine if he or she matched any criminal activity on file, to verify the alien's identity, and to facilitate card production. In addition, biometrics collection enables DHS to confirm that individuals are not utilizing multiple identities or that multiple individuals are not utilizing one identity. Lastly, from biometrics collection DHS would increase program integrity by ensuring that only eligible aliens who continued to pursue asylum were applying for and obtaining work authorization. This would also generally provide a benefit for the public; in that it increases transparency pertinent to application and filing requirements. As discussed in the preamble, the asylum program has been subject to identity fraud concerns historically.
该拟议规则的影响既包括潜在的分配性影响(即转移性影响),也包括成本。潜在的分配性影响落在那些可能被延迟进入美国劳动力市场的庇护申请人身上,或者落在那些可能因不符合资格(例如,严重重罪犯、严重非政治性犯罪等)或因处理暂停而无法获得工作许可文件(EAD)的庇护申请人身上。潜在的分配性影响(转移性影响)将以丧失获得报酬(工资和福利)的机会的形式出现。这部分丧失的报酬中有一部分可能会从庇护申请人转移给目前已在美国劳动力市场中就业的其他人员,可能以增加工时或加班费的形式体现。该规则的部分影响也可能由那些本应雇用庇护申请人的公司承担,如果这些申请人本可以及早获得工作许可文件或进入劳动力市场的话(印刷页第8665页)。然而,如果受影响的雇主无法找到可用的工人,这些公司可能会在生产力和潜在利润方面承担成本。
The impacts of this proposed rule include both potential distributional effects (which are transfers) and costs. The potential distributional impacts fall on the asylum applicants who may be delayed in entering the U.S. labor force or who may not obtain an EAD due to being ineligible ( e.g., aggravated felon, serious non-political crime, etc.) or due to a processing pause. The potential distributional impacts (transfers) would be in the form of lost opportunity to earn compensation (wages and benefits). A portion of this lost compensation might be transferred from asylum applicants to others that are currently employed in the U.S. labor force, possibly in the form of additional hours worked or overtime pay. A portion of the impacts of this rule may also be borne by companies that would have ( printed page 8665) hired the asylum applicants had they been eligible for an EAD or in the labor market earlier. However, if the affected employer were unable to find available workers, these companies could incur a cost to productivity and potential profit.
公司还可能因不得不选择次优方案来立即填补庇护申请人本应填补的职位而产生机会成本。美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)并不知道这些公司的次优方案会是什么。因此,USCIS不清楚该规则总体影响中有多少部分属于转移性影响,有多少部分属于成本。如果公司能够为庇护申请人本应担任的职位找到替代劳动力,该规则的主要影响将是分配性的,即以转移的形式从庇护申请人转移给已经在劳动力市场中的其他人员(或被诱导重返劳动力市场的工人)。USCIS承认,雇主可能会产生额外的机会成本,例如额外的招聘搜寻成本。然而,如果公司无法为庇护申请人本应提供的劳动力找到合理的替代方案,该规则的影响将主要表现为这些公司因生产力和利润损失而产生的成本。
Companies may also incur opportunity costs by having to choose the next best alternative to immediately filling the job the asylum applicant would have filled. USCIS does not know what this next best alternative may be for those companies. As a result, USCIS does not know the portion of overall impacts of this rule that are transfers or costs. If companies can find replacement labor for the position the asylum applicant would have filled, this rule would have primarily distributional effects in the form of transfers from asylum applicants to others already in the labor market (or workers induced to return to the labor market). USCIS acknowledges that there may be additional opportunity costs to employers such as additional search costs. However, if companies cannot find a reasonable substitute for the labor an asylum applicant would have provided, the effect of this rule would primarily be a cost to these companies through lost productivity and profits.
USCIS将庇护申请人收入的变化作为衡量该规则总体影响的指标,无论是作为分配性影响(转移性影响),还是作为企业生产力损失成本的代理指标。该指标并未包括企业因利润损失和机会成本而产生的额外成本,也未包括申请人支持网络中人员所承受的分配性影响。根据庇护申请人本可能获得的工资水平,这些庇护申请人每年可能损失的报酬总额(未贴现)在346亿美元至1266亿美元之间。以3%的贴现率计算,庇护申请人五年期(2025财年至2029财年)贴现后的总损失报酬可能在1554亿美元至5686亿美元之间;以7%的贴现率计算,则可能在1355亿美元至4958亿美元之间。
USCIS uses the changes to earnings to asylum applicants as a measure of the overall impact of the rule—either as distributional impacts (transfers) or as a proxy for businesses' cost for lost productivity. It does not include additional costs to businesses for lost profits and opportunity costs or the distributional impacts for those in an applicant's support network. The lost compensation to these asylum applicants could range from $34.6 billion to $126.6 billion annually (undiscounted) depending on the wages the asylum applicant would have earned. The 5-year total discounted lost compensation to asylum applicants at 3 percent could range from $155.4 billion to $568.6 billion and at 7 percent could range from $135.5 billion to $495.8 billion (FY 2025 through FY 2029).
这些量化估计可能被高估了,因为这些估计假定,在没有本规则的情况下(即在基准情形下),工作许可文件(EAD)的有效期将长于目前所允许的期限。[294]由于USCIS已将有待决庇护申请的外国人的工作许可文件最长有效期缩短至18个月,申请人必须更频繁地续签,这可能导致在五年分析期内获得工作授权的待决庇护申请人数量减少。这种减少将源于续签申请的自然减损以及更频繁的审查核实。
The quantified estimates may be overstated, as they assume that without this rule ( i.e., under the baseline) the EAD validity period would be longer than is currently permitted.[294] Since USCIS has reduced the maximum EAD validity for aliens with pending asylum applications to 18 months, recipients must renew more often, which could result in fewer pending asylum applicants authorized to work over the 5-year period of analysis. This reduction would result from attrition in renewal applications and more frequent vetting.
与收入变化相关,可能存在税收影响。那些可能被延迟或无法获得工作许可文件的庇护申请人,可能会导致联邦和州税收的损失。然而,如上所述,由于本规则的效果而可能缓解的资源压力,可能会抵消部分或全部税收损失(如果确实存在此类损失的话)。此外,如果这些收入转移给了美国工人,则可能不会造成税收损失。
There could be tax impacts pertinent to earnings changes. Asylum applicants who could be delayed or precluded from obtaining an EAD may generate forgone federal and state taxes. However, as was noted above, the strain on resources that could be mitigated due to the effects of this rule could counterbalance some or all of the tax losses, if there are any. Additionally, if the earnings are transferred to American workers, there may no loss of taxes.
该规则可能会减少联邦政府的机会成本。自Rosario案法院命令(365 F. Supp. 3d 1156(W.D. Wash. 2018))迫使美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)在2018财年遵守30天处理时限规定以来,USCIS已重新分配其裁决资源以努力达到合规要求。通过将30天处理时限延长至180天,有可能重新分配资源,从而减少身份授予类福利申请处理的延误,并避免与雇用更多员工相关的成本。然而,有许多因素可能影响此类处理工作。此外,如果本规则对以获得工作许可证(EAD)为目的的无实质理由申请产生抑制作用,从而导致庇护申请数量下降,那么公众和联邦政府可能会因裁决较少的庇护申请而获得与运营和成本效率相关的益处。国土安全部(DHS)不排除可能会将资源分配到其他运营领域的可能性。
This rule could possibly result in reduced opportunity costs to the Federal Government. Since the Rosario court order, 365 F. Supp. 3d 1156 (W.D. Wash. 2018), compelled USCIS to comply with the 30-day processing timeframe provision in FY 2018, USCIS has redistributed its adjudication resources to work up to compliance. By extending the 30-day processing timeframe to 180 days, it is possible that resources could be reallocated, which could have the effect of reducing delays in processing status-granting benefit requests, and avoiding costs associated with hiring additional employees. However, there are many factors that could influence such processing. Additionally, if asylum filings decline, as this rule generates a disincentive to meritless claims with the goal of obtaining an EAD, then the public and the Federal Government could experience operational and cost efficiencies as is pertinent to adjudicating less asylum claims. DHS does not rule out that there could be resources allocated to other operational areas.
表4详细总结了拟议规则各项条款的监管变化及预期影响。USCIS估计,该规则的主要影响将来自于暂停受理所有初次(c)(8)类工作许可证申请,直至USCIS庇护申请(主动申请)的平均处理时间达到180天为止(详见下文模块1的估算)。此外,USCIS还就暂停解除后将影响工作许可证申请人(包括初次申请和续签申请)的各项条款提供了货币化影响估算(详见下文模块2的估算)。但是,USCIS未将模块2纳入本规则的总体影响计算,因为模块1(暂停工作许可证)的影响已涵盖所有新工作许可证申请人的影响。若将模块2一并纳入,将对同一人群的影响造成重复计算。凡列出货币化数字之处,均以7%的年化平均值为基础,年度人数则取高低区间的中位数。
Table 4 provides a detailed summary of the regulatory changes and the expected impacts of the proposed rule's provisions. USCIS estimates the primary impact of the rule would result from a pause in accepting all initial (c)(8) EADs applications until USCIS affirmative asylum applications processing time reaches a 180-day average (estimated in Module 1, below). Additionally, USCIS provides monetized impacts for provisions that would affect EAD applicants (for initial and renewal EADs) when the pause is lifted (estimated in Module 2, below). However, USCIS does not include Module 2 in the total rule impact, because the Module 1 impacts (pause EADs) accounts for impacts to all new EAD applicants. To include Module 2 would be double counting the impacts for the same population. Where a monetized figure is presented, it is based on a 7 percent annualized average, and the annual population is the midpoint of a high-low range.

二、背景与目的
2. Background and Purpose
该拟议规则将影响向有庇护申请待决的外国人签发工作许可证(c)(8)类工作许可证)的程序、(c)(8)类工作许可证申请的处理时限、申请并获得(c)(8)类工作许可证的等待期、(c)(8)类工作许可证的有效期,以及(c)(8)类工作许可证的资格要求。该规则将需要对现有监管文本进行修改,并制定新的监管文本。
The proposed rule would impact the process for issuance of EADs for aliens with a pending asylum application (c)(8) EAD, processing timeframe for (c)(8) EAD applications, waiting period to apply for and receive a (c)(8) EAD, (c)(8) EAD validity period, and eligibility requirements for (c)(8) EADs. The rule will require changes to existing regulatory text and the creation of new regulatory text.
本拟议规则制定的目的在于使国土安全部(DHS)能够平衡其整体裁决负担与可用资源,确保初次(c)(8)类工作许可证申请不会诱使外国人提交轻率、欺诈或其他无实质理由的庇护申请。因此,本规则拟引入若干附加条款,以协助解决福利与项目诚信、国家安全、公共安全以及USCIS资源紧张等问题。DHS预计拟议变更将减少外国人及公众对有庇护申请待决者工作许可证要求的困惑,并有助于确保监管文本反映DHS现行政策,更忠实地落实法律本意,同时提升项目诚信。
The purpose of this proposed rulemaking is for DHS to be able to balance its overall adjudication burdens with available resources by ensuring that initial (c)(8) EAD filings are not creating incentives for aliens to file frivolous, fraudulent, or otherwise meritless asylum applications. Thus, this rule proposes to introduce a number of additional provisions to help with the issues of benefit and program integrity, national security, public safety, and resource strain at USCIS. DHS expects the proposed changes would reduce confusion regarding EAD requirements for aliens with pending asylum claims and the public and help ensure the regulatory text reflects current DHS policy and more faithfully implements the intent of the statute while simultaneously improving program integrity.
三、基准与受影响人群
3. Baseline and Population
该拟议规则将影响向有庇护申请待决的外国人签发就业许可文件(c)(8)类工作许可证)的程序、在最终规则生效之日持有有效工作许可证但庇护申请已被拒绝的外国人、(c)(8)类工作许可证申请的处理时限、申请并获得(c)(8)类工作许可证的等待期、(c)(8)类工作许可证的有效期,以及(c)(8)类工作许可证的资格要求。该规则将需要对现有监管文本进行修改,并制定新的监管文本。
The proposed rule will impact the process for issuance of employment authorization documents for aliens with a pending asylum application (c)(8) EAD, aliens with denied asylum claims who have a valid EAD at the effective date of the final rule, processing timeframe for (c)(8) EAD applications, waiting period to apply for and receive a (c)(8) EAD, (c)(8) EAD validity period, and eligibility requirements for (c)(8) EADs. The rule will require changes to existing regulatory text and the creation of new regulatory text.
本拟议规则制定通知(NPRM)中的基准代表的是在没有本拟议法规的情况下的世界状况,即现行政策与趋势的延续。本监管影响分析(RIA)中估算的各项影响均相对于此基准而言。
The baseline in this NPRM represents a world absent this proposed regulation, which is a continuation of current policy and trends. The impacts estimated in this RIA are relative to this baseline.
本拟议规则制定影响的人群是庇护申请待审人群,即其庇护申请正处于待决状态的人员。在其庇护申请待决期间,该人群可以申请工作许可,俗称(c)(8)类工作许可证(EAD)。就本拟议规则制定通知(NPRM)而言,为预测未来潜在的(c)(8)类工作许可证人群,我们需要考虑任何历史模式。表6列示了初次申请(c)(8)类工作许可证人群的历史情况。总体而言,可以看出该人群在2016财年至2024财年这九年期间以约39.26%的平均速率增长。需要说明的是,2022财年至2024财年的增长率高于2016财年至2024财年整个期间的增长率。若剔除高增长年份,在2017财年至2021财年这五年期间,增长率为7.30%。就本拟议规则制定通知而言,我们将该比率作为该人群长期平均增长率。
The population affected by this proposed rulemaking is the asylum requesting population whose asylum applications are pending. While their asylum applications are pending, this population can request employment authorization, colloquially known as a (c)(8) EAD. For this NPRM, to project a potential future (c)(8) EAD population we need to account for any historical patterns. Table 6 presents the historical perspective of the initial (c)(8) EAD population. On average we can see that this population grew at about a 39.26 percent rate over the nine-year span of period FY 2016 through FY 2024. One note is the growth rates of FY 2022 through FY 2024 were higher than that of period FY 2016 through FY 2024. Excluding the high-growth years, over the five-year period FY 2017 through FY 2021, the growth rate was 7.30 percent. For this NPRM, we take this rate as the average longer term growth rate of this population.
(印刷页第8675页)
( printed page 8675)
美国国土安全部(DHS)无法预测2022财年至2024财年的增长率是否代表一种结构性变化,因为移民政策以及国内外其他情况可能发生变化,从而可能影响庇护申请人。鉴于这种不确定性,在预测未来潜在的(c)(8)类工作许可证人群时,我们考虑了两种可能性。我们首先预测一种情景(表7中的“低情景”),假设由独特因素、政策或两者共同影响所驱动的高增长年份水平不会在未来持续以同样速度发展。我们还依据一种“高情景”,即高增长年份可能构成一种可持续的趋势变化。因此,对于低情景,我们采用2021财年(c)(8)类工作许可证数据点214,565,然后applied应用假定的长期增长率7.3%,得出表7中第一个预测年度,即2025财年的数值。然后我们取预测的2025财年数值,对其余预测年度重复此计算。对于高情景,我们采用高增长年份的平均值,即表6中2022财年至2024财年的平均值,[297]为755,385,然后应用假定的长期增长率7.3%,得出第一个预测年度2025财年的数值。然后我们取预测的2025财年数值,对其余预测年度重复此计算。最后,我们取低情景与高情景的平均值(表7中的“中点情景”),得出中点情景人群数量。
DHS cannot predict whether the FY 2022 through FY 2024 growth rates represent a structural change as immigration policies and other conditions domestically and internationally could change and potentially affect asylum applicants. Given this uncertainty, in projecting a potential future (c)(8) EAD population we account for two possibilities. We first project a scenario (“low scenario” in Table 7) where we assume the levels of the high-growth years driven by unique factors, policies, or some influence of both that would not continue apace into the future. We also rely on a “high scenario” where the high-growth years might constitute a sustainable change in trend. As such, for the low scenario we take the FY 2021 (c)(8) EAD data point, which is 214,565, then apply the assumed longer term growth rate of 7.3 percent to arrive at our first projected year, FY 2025 of Table 7. Then we take the projected FY 2025 and repeat for the remaining projected years. For the high scenario, we take the average of the high-growth years, average of FY 2022 through FY 2024 in Table 6,[297] which is 755,385, then apply the assumed longer term growth rate of 7.3 percent to arrive at our first projected year, FY 2025. Then we take the projected FY 2025 and repeat for the remaining projected years. Lastly, we take the average of the low and high scenarios (the “midpoint scenario” in Table 7) to arrive at a midpoint scenario population.
表7列示了2025财年至2029财年的预测基准人群数量。我们将预测期设定为5年,因为鉴于移民政策以及国内外其他情况可能迅速变化,从而影响庇护申请待决的外国人,我们无法确定地预测更长期的趋势。
Table 7 presents the projected baseline population for FY 2025 through FY 2029. We set our projection period to 5 years as we cannot predict with certainty longer term trends given that immigration policies and other conditions domestically and internationally could rapidly change affecting aliens with pending asylum applications.

( printed page 8676)
与本规则制定具体方面相关的子人群构成部分将在第VI.A.6节中列示。
The sub-population components pertinent to specific aspects of this rulemaking will be presented in Section VI.A.6.
4. 工资与时间机会成本
4. Wages and Opportunity Costs of Time
为了将本拟议规则的影响货币化,我们需要有关基准人群可能获得的工资信息,或其时间机会成本的信息。为估算潜在收入影响,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)按如下方式使用美国劳工统计局(BLS)的数据。我们将使用所有职业的平均时薪31.48美元作为上限,使用第10百分位工资13.97美元作为下限。[298]为获得更全面的薪酬衡量指标,我们将对相应的平均工资和第10百分位工资分别适用1.45的福利乘数,从而得出平均每小时总薪酬率分别为45.65美元和20.26美元。[299]
To monetize the impacts of this proposed rule, we need information on potential wages that the baseline population could earn or information on their opportunity cost of time. To estimate potential earnings impacts, USCIS makes uses of U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) data as follows. We will use the mean hourly wage for all occupations of $31.48 as an upper bound and the 10th percentile wage of $13.97 as a lower bound.[298] For a more encompassing measure of compensation, we will use a benefits multiplier of 1.45 applied to the respective mean and 10th percentile wages, resulting in average hourly total compensation rates of $45.65 and $20.26, respectively.[299]
就劳动收入可能受到的影响而言,国土安全部(DHS)还估计了潜在的税收效应。由于个人纳税情况差异很大,量化劳动力市场情景下就业所产生的所得税影响具有挑战性,但国土安全部估计了对就业税,即医疗保险税和社会保障税的潜在贡献性影响,二者合计税率为7.65%（分别为6.2%和1.45%）。[300] 由于雇员和雇主均需分别缴纳各自应承担的医疗保险税和社会保障税部分,雇员和雇主向医疗保险和社会保障缴纳的税收转移支付总增加额估计为15.3%。国土安全部以未计负担的收入为基础估算税收影响。其计算方法为将收入影响乘以15.3%的就业税率,再将所得乘积除以1.45的福利负担倍数。国土安全部无法量化其他税收转移支付,例如适用于联邦所得税以及州和地方税的部分。
As it relates to potential impacts to labor earnings, DHS also estimates a potential tax effect. It is challenging to quantify income tax impacts of employment in the labor market scenario because individual tax situations vary widely, but DHS estimates the potential contributory effects on employment taxes, namely Medicare and Social Security, which have a combined tax rate of 7.65 percent (6.2 percent and 1.45 percent, respectively).[300] With both the employee and employer paying their respective portion of Medicare and Social Security taxes, the total estimated accretion in tax transfer payments from employees and employers to Medicare and Social Security is 15.3 percent. DHS estimates the tax impacts on the unburdened earnings basis. This is calculated by multiplying the earnings impact by the employment tax rate of 15.3 percent, and dividing the resulting product by the benefits burden multiple of 1.45. DHS is unable to quantify other tax transfer payments, such as those applicable to Federal income taxes and State and local taxes.
5. 表格、时间负担和费用
5. Forms, Time Burdens, and Fees
直至最近,申请庇护或初次(c)(8)类工作许可文件（EAD）均无需缴纳任何费用,且(c)(8)类EAD申请也不要求采集生物特征信息。美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）近期实施了法定强制收费规定,包括对庇护申请收取100美元不可豁免的申请费,以及申请人庇护申请待决期间每年收取100美元年费,此外对初次(c)(8)类工作许可申请收取550美元不可豁免的申请费。[301] 本拟议规则规定对(c)(8)类EAD申请实行生物特征信息采集,但本规则制定并未就此征收服务费。国土安全部已在本分析的最后一节中说明了与生物特征信息采集相关的其他影响。本规则制定拟议对I-589表和I-765表（与基线人群相关的表格）进行修改。目前,I-589表的填表负担为11小时,I-765表的填表负担为4.38小时。[302] 国土安全部估计,未来I-765表的填报时间将为4.72小时。在影响评估中,国土安全部将采用这一预测负担值,并指出这代表了最大影响。（印刷页第8677页）原因在于部分申请人以电子方式提交申请,电子申请的负担将较低,其时间将从4.12小时变为4.35小时。[303]
Until recently, there were no fees associated with requesting asylum or an initial (c)(8) EAD, and no biometrics collection requirement associated with (c)(8) EAD applications. USCIS recently implemented statutorily-mandated filing fees, including a $100 non-waivable filing fee for the asylum application and $100 annual fee for every year the applicant's asylum application is pending, as well as a $550 non-waivable filing fee for the initial (c)(8) employment authorization application.[301] The proposed rule establishes a biometrics collection applicable to (c)(8) EAD requests, but it is not imposing a service fee via this rulemaking. DHS explained other impacts linked to biometrics collection in the final section of this analysis. This rulemaking proposes changes to the forms I-589 and I-765, forms relevant to the baseline population. The current form burdens are 11 hours for Form I-589 and 4.38 hours for Form I-765.[302] DHS estimates that the future filing time for Form I-765 will be 4.72 hours. In the impact estimates, DHS will rely on this projected burden, noting that it represents the maximum impact. The ( printed page 8677) reason is that some aliens file electronically, and the burden for electronic filings will be less, changing from 4.12 hours to 4.35 hours.[303]
在影响评估中,国土安全部考虑了与申请及不申请EAD相关变化所涉及的成本和成本节约,但未将近期法定强制收费规定纳入这些评估之中。
In the impact estimates, DHS accounts for costs and cost-savings applicable to changes involving filing for- and not filing for, an EAD, but does not include the recent statutorily-mandated filing fees in these estimates.
6. 货币化影响（成本、收益和转移支付）
6. Monetized Impacts (Costs, Benefits, and Transfers)
a. 变量及说明
a. Variables and Descriptions
在本节中,国土安全部就拟议规则可能产生的潜在经济影响进行了阐述、估算、量化并货币化,尽管并非所有影响均能完全量化。主要影响为已获得(c)(8)类EAD的庇护申请人的劳动报酬收入可能发生的变化。收入方面的变化很可能体现为EAD持有人收入的延迟或损失,但汇总收入变化中的部分乃至全部,也可能构成向美国工人的转移支付,而不会对雇主造成生产力损失。国土安全部承认,(c)(8)类续签申请也会产生相应影响,但为限定本分析的范围,我们将重点放在初次获批的(c)(8)类EAD这一主要影响上。截至本分析撰写之时,即2025年6月,初次(c)(8)类EAD的有效期为五年,这也是确定分析期限的主要依据。[304] 除收入影响外,还可能存在税收影响,以及未来不申请EAD的庇护申请人所产生的成本和成本节约,以及表格填报负担的变化。上述影响也已在可能的范围内进行了量化。
In this section DHS develops, estimates, quantifies, and monetizes potential economic impacts that could accrue to the proposed rule, although not all impacts can be fully quantified. The primary effect will be changes to labor compensation earnings that asylum applicants who have obtained (c)(8) EADs could incur. The changes to earnings would likely comprise delayed or forgone earnings to EAD holders; however, some portion, or the totality of the aggregate earnings change could also constitute transfers to American workers without productivity loss to employers. DHS acknowledges that there would be impacts applicable to (c)(8) renewal filings, but to scope the analysis, we focus on the primary impact of an initial approved (c)(8) EAD. At the time of this analysis, June 2025, an initial (c)(8) EAD is authorized for five years, which is the main basis for the analysis period.[304] In addition to earnings impacts there could be tax impacts as well as costs and cost-savings to asylum applicants not filing for EADs in the future and changes to the form burden. These effects are also quantified, to the extent possible.
货币化估计影响分为两个模块进行测算。模块1涵盖暂停签发初次(c)(8)类就业许可文件(EAD),直至美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)积极型庇护申请处理时间达到180天平均水平为止所适用的影响。模块2涵盖与EAD资格相关的规定、提前终止部分EAD,以及拟议的对(c)(8)申请时限和处理时间协议所作变更。对初次(c)(8)类EAD申请人资格要求的变更,只有在模块1所涉及的暂停措施解除后方才适用。之所以采用这种方式进行拆分,是为避免重复计算,因为签发暂停措施将构成最大的影响,并涵盖在签发协议(模块2)下可能产生的任何影响。本分析中使用了大量指标和输入数据,为简明起见,我们将对许多指标使用字母缩写,这些缩写将在适用时予以介绍。
The monetized estimated impacts are developed in two modules. Module 1 covers the impacts applicable to a pause in the issuance of initial (c)(8) EADs until USCIS affirmative asylum applications processing time reaches a 180-day average. Module 2 covers the provisions related to EAD eligibility, ending some EADs early, and well as the proposed changes to the (c)(8) filing time and process time protocol. The changes to EAD eligibility requirements for initial (c)(8) EAD applicants would become applicable only when the pause, covered by Module 1, is lifted. The impacts are parsed this way to avoid double counting, as an issuance pause would comprise the largest impact and encompass any effects that could be incurred under an issuance protocol (Module 2). There are numerous metrics and inputs utilized in the analysis, and for the purpose of brevity, we will utilize letter abbreviations for many, which will be introduced when applicable.
为支持本拟议规则的经济影响估算,美国国土安全部(DHS)分析了美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)绩效与质量办公室提供的数据。该数据集将庇护申请信息与相应的(c)(8)类EAD申请信息相关联,涵盖2022财年至2024财年全期共226万条记录。[305]该数据集完全涵盖了USCIS积极型庇护数据,虽然其中包含与防御型庇护相关联的EAD数据,但有关防御型庇护结果的数据尚不完整,因为DHS目前尚无法将USCIS与行政复议办公室(EOIR)之间的此类数据进行串联。
To support the economic impact estimates for this proposed rule, DHS analyzed data provided by the USCIS Office of Performance and Quality. The data set links information on asylum filings with concomitant (c)(8) EAD filings, comprising 2.26 million records for the full period FY 2022 through FY 2024.[305] It fully embodies USCIS affirmative asylum data, and while it includes EAD data linked to defensive asylum, data regarding defensive asylum outcomes are not fully available, as DHS cannot concatenate such data between USCIS and EOIR, at this time.
总体而言,总人口(POP)是指初次(c)(8)类EAD获批的总人数,因为被拒者不会受到影响。分析的第一阶段拆分并报告了本规则可能影响的具体人群,如表8所示。在模块1和模块2下,总体人群均可能受到影响,但在估算影响的方式上存在重要区别。如果出现初次(c)(8)类EAD签发暂停(即模块1),则整个人群,即正在等待积极型和防御型庇护申请结果的初次EAD申请人,均将受到影响,因为相关个人均无法获得EAD。相反,当暂停解除时(即模块2),DHS假设初次(c)(8)类EAD将主要签发给待决的防御型庇护申请人。这是因为只有当积极型庇护处理时间少于180天时,暂停才会解除,而这通常发生在拟议的365天初次(c)(8)类EAD等待期结束之前。庇护申请获批将授予工作许可,而被拒则使该个人不具备工作许可资格。
The population (POP) at its broad level is the total population of initial (c)(8) EAD approvals, as denials would not be impacted. A first phase of the analysis parses out and reports the specific populations that the rule could impact, as is shown in Table 8. Under both Module 1 and 2 the broad population could be impacted, but there is an important distinction in the manner in which the impacts are estimated. If there is a pause in the issuance of initial (c)(8) EADs ( i.e., Module 1), the entire population, initial EAD filings for both pending affirmative and defensive asylum would be impacted, as no pertinent individuals would obtain an EAD. In contrast, when the pause is lifted ( i.e., Module 2), DHS assumes that initial (c)(8) EADs would be issued primarily for pending defensive asylum applicants. This is because the pause is lifted only when the processing time for affirmative asylum processing is less than 180 days, and thus prior to the end of the proposed 365-day waiting period for an initial (c)(8) EAD. An asylum approval would grant work authorization, and a denial would make the individual ineligible for work authorization.
如序言中所述,鉴于积极型庇护待决案件的规模,USCIS已准备好在本规则生效后暂停签发(c)(8)类初次EAD。如序言前文所述,(c)(8)类初次EAD申请数量的减少可能成为缩短庇护处理时间的一个因素,这可能使USCIS未来能够达到180天庇护平均处理时间的监管标准,从而解除暂停,重新开始签发(c)(8)类初次EAD。尽管DHS无法推测这将在何时发生,但根据此前讨论的估算,DHS假设这很可能会超出用于估算暂停相关影响的五年期限。因此,为说明监管基准起见,DHS可以假设,一旦模块2启动,在不存在拟议的对时限和资格限制所作变更的情况下,庇护申请及(c)(8)类EAD的数量将恢复到本规则(及暂停)实施之前的水平。
As is explained in the preamble, given the scope of the pending affirmative asylum caseload, USCIS is prepared to pause (c)(8) initial EADs when the rule becomes effective. As discussed earlier in the preamble, the reduction in (c)(8) initial EAD filings could be a factor in reducing process times for asylum, which could position USCIS in the future to meet the regulatory criteria of 180-day asylum average process time and lift the pause to begin again issuing (c)(8) initial EADs. While DHS cannot speculate on when this would occur, based on estimates discussed previously, DHS assumes it would likely be outside the five-year span under which the impacts applicable to the pause are estimated. Therefore, for purposes of explaining a regulatory baseline, DHS could assume that once Module 2 commences, in the absence of the proposed changes to the clock and the eligibility bars, asylum and (c)(8) EAD volumes would revert back to levels before the rule (and pause) took place.
上述讨论表明,如果国土安全部(DHS)能够满足180天的庇护审理时限要求,则适用于肯定性庇护的(c)(8)初始工作许可文件(EAD)数量可能会趋近于零。这对肯定性庇护的影响是双重的;首先,肯定性申请获批的个人可以从中受益,因为他们可能更早获得工作许可,即他们的庇护申请会在其“过去”的申请或工作许可文件(以两者中较早获批者为准)获批之前获得批准。其次,个人将因无需申请工作许可文件而节省相关费用。尽管国土安全部预计会出现这些影响,但出于以下两个原因,本部未对肯定性人群的影响进行估算;首先,如上文所述,作为基线,我们本可以假设庇护申请及相应的(c)(8)工作许可文件数量将恢复至本规则实施前的水平,但这并不现实。由于(最初的)暂停措施所产生的持续影响,与模块二相关的初始(c)(8)数量可能会与近期历史数量存在较大差异。其次,国土安全部无法预测庇护申请将在(180天期限内)何时获批。综上所述,国土安全部承认本拟议规则将对庇护人群及相关工作许可文件人群产生重大影响,但由于上述原因,国土安全部无法量化潜在的提前获得收入和申请费用节省所带来的间接影响。
What the above discussion suggests is that if DHS is meeting the 180-day asylum processing time, (c)(8) initial EAD volumes applicable to affirmative asylum could converge to zero. The impact to affirmative asylum is twofold; first, individuals with approved affirmative claims could benefit because they could become work authorized earlier—their asylum claim would be approved before their “past” claim or EAD, whichever of the latter two was approved first. Second, individuals would realize a cost-savings from not filing for an EAD. While DHS expects these impacts to occur, the Department does not make estimates of the impacts to the affirmative population for two reasons; first, as was stated above, for a baseline we could assume asylum filings and concomitant (c)(8) EADs would revert to pre-rule levels, but this is not realistic. Due to lingering effects of the (initial) pause the initial (c)(8) volumes relevant to Module 2 would likely be quite different than recent historical volumes. Second, DHS cannot predict when an asylum claim could be approved (within the 180-day window). In summary, DHS acknowledges that this proposed rule will have a significant impact on both the asylum and associated EAD populations but, for the reasons described above, DHS is unable to quantify the indirect impacts of potential earlier earnings and filing cost-savings.
在模块二中,国土安全部的假设前提是,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)受理的肯定性庇护申请人通常不会申请工作许可文件,因为其庇护案件可能会在工作许可文件申请获批之前作出裁决。鉴于工作许可文件(印刷页第8678页)申请计时从150天更改为360天,这种可能性进一步增加。因此,国土安全部假设肯定性人群不会受到模块二条款的影响。对于模块二,国土安全部对防御性人群(也称为移民法院审查执行办公室(EOIR)人群)所受影响进行了估算。防御性案件将不受拟议的180天庇护审理时限要求的约束。因此,资格限制条款及计时变更可能会对防御性案件产生影响。原因在于,数据分析报告显示,美国公民及移民服务局移交移民法院审查执行办公室案件的中位时间(即从收到庇护申请之日至移交裁决作出之日)为231天。将该移交时间加上移民法院审查执行办公室目前冗长的庇护案件审理时间,国土安全部预计,即使工作许可文件计时发生变更,大多数人仍将从申请工作许可文件中受益,因为该许可将在庇护裁决作出之前获批。
In Module 2, DHS operates under the assumption that USCIS affirmative asylum applicants would generally not file for an EAD, as their asylum case would potentially be decided in advance of their application for an EAD. This is even more likely given the EAD ( printed page 8678) filing clock change from 150 to 360 days. Therefore, DHS assumes the affirmative population would not be affected by the Module 2 provision. For Module 2, DHS estimates impacts to the defensive population, also described as the EOIR population. Defensive cases would not be subject to the proposed 180-day asylum process time requirement. Hence the eligibility bars and clock changes would potentially be impactful to defensive cases. The reason is that the data analysis reports the median time for a USCIS referral to EOIR (date of receipt of the asylum application to the referral decision date) is 231 days. Adding the referral time frame to the lengthy current process time for asylum cases at EOIR, DHS expects that most would benefit from filing for an EAD as it would be approved before the asylum decision date, even with the EAD clock changes.
为进行本项分析,国土安全部从初始工作许可文件申请记录中随机抽取了5,000份样本,其中包括肯定性和防御性庇护案件。该样本量远大于获得95%置信度结果所需的规模,但由于受影响的子人群众多,国土安全部大幅扩大了抽样规模,以确保各群体之间获得充分代表性。人群构成情况见表8,该表基于2025财政年度至2029财政年度分析期间的预计年化平均申请量编制而成。需要说明的是,所涉及的总体人群数量是以受理量(见第六节A.3部分)乘以83.5%的批准率(适用于肯定性和防御性申请)计算得出的,该比率为2019财政年度至2024财政年度期间(c)(8)工作许可文件批准率的加权平均值(在估算具体影响时,国土安全部采用了一个基于历史批准率数据得出的区间,即APV范围)。
To conduct the analysis, DHS drew a random sample of 5,000 records from initial EAD applications, including affirmative and defensive asylum cases. This size is much larger than is required to generate 95 percent confidence in the results, but DHS oversampled considerably because there are multiple sub-populations impacted, and DHS sought assurance of adequate inter-group representation. The population breakout is reported in Table 8 and is based on the projected annualized average filing volumes or the analysis period of FY 2025 to FY 2029. It is noted that the encompassing population comprises receipt volumes (Section VI.A.3) multiplied by an approval rate of 83.5 percent (applicable to both affirmative and defensive filings), which is the weighted average (c)(8) EAD approval rate for the period FY 2019 through FY 2024 (in estimating specific impacts DHS utilizes a range, derived from historical data for the approval rate, APV).
(印刷页第8679页)
( printed page 8679)
从样本中得出的比例份额被推算至总体人口，以获得受影响外国人数量的估计值。对于模块二，A行的基础是EOIR总人数，根据我们的分析，该人数占已批准(c)(8)类就业许可文件（EAD）估计人口的63.7%。[307] 国土安全部（DHS）审查了三项拟议的资格限制条款：一年申请期限、未经检查入境、以及犯罪限制条款。对于一年申请期限，国土安全部计算了入境日期与收到I-589表格之间的天数间隔，以获得可能受一年申请期限限制条款（OYB）约束的人口估计值。国土安全部还筛选了记录为未经检查入境（EWI）的案件，以估计可能受EWI限制条款约束的潜在人口。从表8可以推算出，35.4%的人可能受到任一限制条款的约束，但我们扣除了重叠校正系数（OCF）5.4%，后者是指两项限制条款都可能适用的百分比，从而得出所示的总份额（A4）。A5行报告的未来EAD申请人总数，为EOIR案件总人数（A行）减去不含重叠部分的OYB和EWI限制条款人口（A4行）。对于其他限制条款，国土安全部无法估计案件数量或比例。犯罪活动信息可能记录在裁决记录和官员笔记中，虽然国土安全部确实掌握了一些因此类理由而被拒绝的庇护案件的数据和信息，但国土安全部没有以适合分析的方式将这些案件与EAD数据相关联的数据。因此，A1、A2和A4中的百分比适用于EOIR总人数。作为参考，适用于(c)(8)类EAD总人口的资格限制条款份额（A4）以及未来将申请的份额（A5）分别为19.1%和44.6%。[308]
The reported shares derived from the sample are extrapolated to the population to obtain estimates of the number of aliens potentially impacted. For Module 2, the basis for Row A is the total EOIR population, which, based on our analysis, is 63.7 percent of the estimated approved (c)(8) EAD population.[307] DHS examined the three proposed bars to eligibility: one-year filing deadline, entry without inspection, and criminal bars. For the one-year filing deadline, DHS calculated the day-duration between the entry date and the receipt of the Form I-589 to obtain an estimate of the population potentially subject to the 1-year filing deadline bar (OYB). DHS also filtered cases recording entry without inspection (EWI) to estimate the potential population that could be subject to the EWI bar. As can be derived from Table 8, 35.4 percent could be subject to either bar, but we removed the overlap correction factor (OCF) of 5.4 percent, the latter of which is the percentage to which both bars could apply, to arrive at the total share shown (A4). The total number of future EAD filers reported in Row A5, is the EOIR cases population (Row A) minus the OYB and EWI bar population excluding overlap (Row A4). For the other bars, DHS cannot make an estimate of the number or share of cases. Information on criminal activity can be recorded in adjudicative records and officer notes and, while DHS does have some data and information asylum cases that were denied on such grounds, DHS does not have data linking those cases to EAD data in a manner suitable for analysis. The percentages in A1, A2, and A4 thus apply to the EOIR population. For reference, the shares applicable to the total (c)(8) EAD population for the eligibility bars (A4) and those that would file in the future (A5) are 19.1 and 44.6 percent, in order.[308]
国土安全部还审查了拟议变更对终止就业授权的影响。159这一数字（表8，B行）是美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）年度庇护拒绝（DEN）中个人已获得EAD的估计数量。本拟议规则将在USCIS拒绝正面庇护申请时终止EAD（在其到期日之前）。国土安全部无法确定本规则生效时有多少EAD仍然有效，因此这一较小数值将作为生效日期受影响数量的替代指标。[309]
DHS also examined the impacts of the proposed changes to termination of employment authorization. The figure of 159 (Row B, Table 8) is the estimated number of annual USCIS asylum denials (DEN) in which the individual received an EAD. This proposed rule would end the EAD (before its expiration date) when USCIS denied the affirmative asylum claim. DHS cannot determine how many would be valid when the rule becomes effective and hence this small volume will be a proxy for the number impacted at effective date.[309]
由于两个模块涉及多个指标，且某些指标存在数值范围，国土安全部将采用基于大量随机种子试验的建模和模拟方法。这种方法提供了一种稳健且高效的估计机制；尽管按照监管指南的要求，模块和类型的影响是分别报告的，但其设置可以嵌套到单一维度的模拟中，以确保影响估计基于相同的随机试验值来处理共同变量（例如，人口数量和(c)(8)批准率）。当涉及数据范围时，若存在最小值、平均值和最大值，则采用三角形数据结构。如果没有平均值或“最可能”值，则采用以最大值和最小值为界的均匀范围，这实质上意味着在试验运行中，该范围内所有数值被选中的概率相同。
Because there are multiple metrics involved in the two modules, and because some incur ranges, DHS will utilize a modelling and simulation approach based on a large number of randomized seed trials. This approach provides a robust and efficient estimation mechanism; even though the impacts across module and type are reported separately per regulatory guidance, their setup can be nested into a single-dimension simulation that ensures the impact estimates are based on the same randomized trial values for common variables (for example, population and (c)(8) approval rate). When a data range is involved, a triangle data structure is utilized when a minimum, average, and maximum value is applicable. If there is not an average or “likeliest” value, a uniform range is bounded with a maximum and minimum value, which essentially means that the probability of any value chosen in a trial run is the same for all values within the range.
国土安全部认为，将劳动力市场的实际情况纳入国土安全部的估计中是适当的。例如，我们可以假设所有持有EAD的个人都将进入劳动力市场，但不能合理地假设他们在规则生效日期时均已就业，因此将失业率纳入考虑是适当的。为整合劳动力市场效应，国土安全部使用以下公式计算强度标量（SCL），即劳动力中每名成员每日所赚取工资小时数的衡量指标：
DHS believes it is appropriate to incorporate realistic aspects of the labor market into the DHS estimates. For example, we can assume that all individuals with EADs would be in the labor force but cannot reasonably assume that all are employed at the effective date of the rule, and thus it would be appropriate to take the unemployment rate into account. To integrate labor market effects, DHS calculates an intensity scalar (SCL), a measure of the hours of wages earned per day, per member of the workforce, using the following equation:

将各项现实劳动因素纳入单一数值进行处理具有优势,因为无需按顺序甚至直接调整各项变量,例如工资的福利负担比率和占总人口的批准率;所有输入项均以乘法方式相互作用,因此可以嵌套为单一维度系统。在描述了数据和人口情况之后,我们接下来进行模块1的估算。
Working with a single value to incorporate realistic labor factors is beneficial because it is not necessary to adjust the variables—such as the benefits burden to wages and the approval rate to the population—sequentially or even directly; all the inputs interact multiplicatively and can therefore be nested as a single dimensional system. Having described the data and the population, we proceed to the Module 1 estimation.
b. 模块1:EAD申请受理暂停
b. Module 1: EAD Application Acceptance Pause
如果(c)(8)类EAD申请受理出现暂停,对外国人的主要影响将是劳动收入的损失,[313]本分析中以“ENG”表示该收入变化。我们将收入变化归因于5年期限,以反映分析进行时的EAD有效期。该数值包含一项调整,以考虑可能转而申请其他类别EAD的情况(以“SUB”表示)。美国国土安全部(DHS)的分析显示,在数据覆盖期内,16.3%的(c)(8)类EAD持有人同时也获得了另一资格类别的EAD。然而,该比例未来可能存在差异。一方面,(c)(8)类EAD申请的暂停可能会促使部分外国人寻求其他资格类别的EAD,从而提高该比例(进而减少会经历收入变化的比例)。但另一方面,如果采取措施减少了那些同时获得双重EAD的其他类别的资格,也可能出现相反的趋势,使该比例下降。因此,我们将SUB比例的上限设定为当前比例(即16.3%的最大值),但允许最低5%的替代比例,前提假设是已在其他资格类别获得EAD的外国人不会遭受收入影响。(第8680页印刷版)
If there is a pause in the acceptance of applications for (c)(8) EADs, the primary impact to aliens would be forgone labor earnings,[313] with the change to earnings denoted ENG throughout this analysis. We attribute the earnings change to a 5-year horizon, to capture the EAD validity period at the time the analysis was conducted. It includes an adjustment that accounts for possible substitution into another EAD category (SUB). DHS analysis reveals that for the data coverage period, 16.3 percent of (c)(8) EAD holders also received an EAD in another category of eligibility. There could be variance to this share in the future, however. On one hand, a pause in (c)(8) EAD applications would likely drive some aliens to seek EADs in other classes of eligibility, thus raising the share (and thereby reducing the share that would experience earnings change). However, a countervailing motion could occur if actions are undertaken that reduce eligibility in other classes that incurred dual EADs, in which the share could drop. Therefore, we will bound the SUB share at the current share (as the maximum, 16.3 percent) but allow as few as 5 percent to substitute, under the assumption that aliens who received an ( printed page 8680) EAD in another eligibility category would not sustain an earnings impact.
收入指标基于小时工资(HWG,范围为13.97美元至31.48美元,未计入负担)计算,时间则以年为单位(YER=365天)。将上文所述的每日工作时间强度标量记为“SCL”,则总影响的估算方程为三项之和。第一项为收入变化,其后为税款变化(TAX),
The earnings metric is based on the hourly wage (HWG, which ranges from $13.97 to $31.48, unburdened) and the time is based on a year (YER = 365 days). Denoting the daily worktime intensity scalar, developed above, as “SCL” the estimating equation for the total impact is the sum of three terms. The first, is the earnings change, followed by changes in taxes (TAX),
(2) ENG = {POP × APV × HWG × (1 − SUB) × SCL × YER};
(2) ENG = {POP × APV × HWG × (1 − SUB) × SCL × YER};
如上所示,TAX的计算方法为收入乘以税率(TXR=15.3%,即医疗保险税与社会保障税之和,详见第4节),再除以福利负担(“BEN”),因为DHS是基于未计入负担的工资来量化税收影响的。第三项为表格负担成本节省额(CSV),表达式为,
As is shown, TAX is calculated as earnings multiplied by the tax rate (TXR=15.3 percent, which is the sum of the Medicare and Social Security tax, developed in Section 4) and divided by the benefits burden (“BEN”) as DHS quantifies tax impacts on unloaded wages. The third term is the form burden cost-savings (CSV), and is expressed as,
(4) CSV = {POP × APV × HWG × (1 − SUB) × FMB × BEN},
(4) CSV = {POP × APV × HWG × (1 − SUB) × FMB × BEN},
其中包含表格时间负担(“FMB”=4.72小时)和BEN。成本节省源于避免了因填写I-765表而产生的时间机会成本。
which includes the form time-burden (“FMB”=4.72 hours) and BEN. Cost-savings accrue from avoided opportunity costs of time for filing the I-765.
DHS将估算设置简化为一个简缩方程,并嵌入模拟程序中。[314]
DHS abridged the estimating setup into a truncated equation and nested in the simulation program.[314]
表10列出了2025财年至2029财年这五年期间的数值。由于模拟输出提供的是平均年度EAD人口的影响,DHS据此计算出五年期的总影响。由于目前EAD有效期为5年,收入和税收影响每年都会连续增长,因为从第二年起会出现人口重叠的情况。[317]然而,对于因初始EAD申请人无法再提交申请而产生的一次性表格提交所带来的成本节省,DHS在每个分析年度中使用的是平均年度EAD人口数(见表8)。
Table 10 reports the values for the 5-year period FY 2025 through FY 2029. Because the simulation output provides the impact for the average annual EAD population, DHS calculated a five-year total impact from this output. Since the EAD length is currently 5 years, the earnings and taxes impacts grow consecutively each year as there are overlapping populations starting in the second year.[317] However, for costs savings that would result from one-time form submission per initial EAD applicant who can no longer file, DHS used the average annual EAD population (Table 8) for each analysis year.

The net impact is earnings minus cost-savings. The results are presented first in undiscounted terms and then at discount rates of 3 and 7 percent, in order.

As is reported in Table 10, net impacts from Module 1 could range from $172,867.8 million to $632,576.3.6 million (undiscounted), with a mean estimate of $368,437.9 million over 5 years, with annualized averages that could range from $34,573.6 million to $126,515.3 million, with a mean of $73,687.6 million. At a 3 percent discount rate, net impacts could range from $155,215.0 million to $567,979.0 million, with a mean estimate of $330,813.9 million over 5 years, with annualized figures es that could range from $33,891.9 million to $124,020.8 million, with a mean of $72,234.7 million. 在完成对(c)(8)类就业许可文件(EAD)签发暂停这一量化及货币化潜在影响的分析和报告后,我们转向模块2的影响分析。
Having developed and reported the quantified and monetized potential impacts of a (c)(8) EAD issuance pause, we turn to Module 2 impacts.
c. 模块2:就业许可文件(EAD)签发条款
c. Module 2: EAD Issuance Provisions
在美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)于180天内处理完毕积极庇护申请的情形下,即USCIS可根据本规则的规定签发(c)(8)类EAD的情形下,本拟议规则的其他条款可能对积极庇护申请外国人及移民法院(EOIR)防御性庇护申请外国人均产生影响。为避免重复计算,模块1与模块2分别予以分析,因为模块1量化的影响构成本规则最大的(总体)影响。如上文关于受影响人群的讨论所述,所有初次获批的(c)(8)类EAD均将受到禁止条款及申请时限变更的约束,但在实践中,此类禁止条款通常仅适用于防御性庇护申请人。这是因为,在初次(c)(8)类EAD签发未被暂停的情况下,USCIS通常会在此类申请人有资格基于未决庇护申请获得工作许可之前,已对积极庇护申请作出裁决。本节中量化的估计值并非在全面暂停情形下估计值的基础上另行叠加,而应视为单独估算结果,以避免重复计算。
Under a scenario in which USCIS is processing affirmative asylum applications within 180 days, and therefore USCIS could issue (c)(8) EADs under the provisions of this rule, there could be impacts from other provisions of the proposed rule to both affirmative asylum aliens and also EOIR defensive aliens. Modules 1 and 2 are parsed separately to avoid double counting, as Module 1 quantified impacts comprise the largest (total) impacts of the rule. As was discussed above in reference to the population, all initial approved (c)(8) EADs would be subject to the bars and filing clock changes but in practice, such bars will generally apply only to defensive asylum applicants. This is because when there is not a pause in the issuance of initial (c)(8) EADs, USCIS would generally be adjudicating affirmative applications for asylum before such applicants are eligible to receive employment authorization on the basis of a pending asylum application. The quantified estimates in this section are not in addition to those under a full pause; they should be considered as separate to not overcount.
EAD拒绝
EAD Denials
拟议规则将导致部分EAD因新要求而提前终止,从而导致工作许可被提前拒绝。关于EAD在其有效期截止日期之前提前终止的情形,正如在适用于受影响人群部分的讨论中所解释的那样,美国国土安全部(DHS)无法确知在特定时间点将有多少案件适用此情形,但将使用年度数字(DEN=159)作为替代指标(根据现有数据,可将其视为在一年内任何时间点有效的案件数量)。[318] 该数字仅适用于相应的积极庇护拒绝案件,若将防御性案件纳入计算,实际数字将更高。为获得相关的收入指标,DHS计算了截至庇护申请被拒绝之日,EAD有效期剩余的时间(TMR),中位数为434天。如上文适用于模块1的部分所述,USCIS近期将(c)(8)类EAD最长有效期限限制为18个月的政策变更,预计也将在此处产生影响。具体而言,剩余时间中位数可能大幅低于434天,这意味着下文就本项影响(收入及税款)所报告的量化估计值目前被高估。以下汇总方程式表示表11中所示的年度结果,涉及因新要求而提前终止EAD所产生的收入及税款,
The proposed rule would result in some EADs ending early due to new requirements, which would result in earlier denial of work authorization. For the ending of EADs earlier than that stipulated in their validity date, as was explained in the discussion applicable to the population, DHS does not know how many cases will apply at a specific point in time but will utilize the annual number (DEN=159) as a proxy (which can be considered the number, based on the data available, that would be valid at any time within a year).[318] This figure only applies to concomitant affirmative asylum denials, and the true figure, inclusive if defensive cases, would be larger. To obtain the pertinent earnings metric, DHS calculated the amount of time remaining (TMR) left in validity at the date of the asylum claim denial, which is a median 434 days. As was noted in the above section applicable to Module 1, the recent USCIS policy change limiting maximum validity for (c)(8) EADs to 18 months will likely have an impact here as well. Specifically, the median remaining time left would potentially be much lower than 434 days, meaning that the quantified estimates reported below for this impact (earnings and taxes) are overstated currently. The following summary equations represent the Annual Results as shown in Table 11 for earnings and taxes based on ending EADs early due to new requirements,
(5) ENG2 = {DEN × TMR × HWG × SCL};
(5) ENG2 = {DEN × TMR × HWG × SCL};
资格禁止条款与EAD时限
Eligibility Bars and EAD Clock
对于移民法院(EOIR)防御性庇护案件,将有两类受影响人群。首先,申请禁止条款将适用,且EAD时限(见印刷版第8684页)的变更将具有约束力。如在受影响人群部分的介绍中所述,DHS预计EAD申请仍将继续提出,因为在目前积压案件庞大的情况下,EAD通常会在庇护申请裁决之前获得批准。首先,我们将介绍用于估算部分EAD提前终止及申请禁止条款所产生影响的估算结构。就后者而言,收入影响的估算方式如下,
For the EOIR defensive asylum cases, there will be two population groups impacted. First, the filing bars will apply, and the changes to the EAD clock ( printed page 8684) will be binding. As was introduced in the presentation of the population, DHS expects EAD filings to continue, as the EAD would generally be approved prior to the adjudication of the asylum claim, at least under the current conditions of large backlogs. First, we present the estimation structure for the impacts that would accrue to the ending of some EADs early and the filing bars. As is applicable to the latter, earnings impacts are estimated as,
(7) ENG3 = POP × APV × (OYB + EWI−OCF) × HWG × SCL × YER。
(7) ENG3 = POP × APV × (OYB + EWI−OCF) × HWG × SCL × YER.
在此方程式中，总体人数为防御性庇护/EOIR（行政复议办公室）人数（见表8），（OYB + EWI−OCF）一项为受禁止条款约束的百分比（占比），税额的计算方式与方程式2相同。可能受资格禁止条款影响的个人也许会申请EAD（就业许可文件），但如果他们提出申请，将会被拒绝。一些外国人可能会意识到自己不符合资格，因而不会提出申请。USCIS（美国公民及移民服务局）认为，可以合理地假设，有法律代理人的潜在申请人很可能会被告知不要提出申请。USCIS对(c)(8)类别的数据进行了评估，确定平均约有一半的人在提出(c)(8) EAD申请时使用代理人。因此，约有一半人可能因不提出申请而节省申请成本，约有一半人则会因提出将被拒绝的EAD申请而产生沉没成本。总体而言，由于成本会抵消节省部分，其净影响接近于零。[319]
In this equation, the population is the defensive asylum/EOIR population (see Table 8), the term (OYB + EWI−OCF) is the percentage (share) subject to the bars, and taxes are calculated the same way as in Equation 2. Individuals that could be impacted by the eligibility bars might file for an EAD, but if they do, they will be denied. Some aliens are likely to realize they are ineligible and will therefore not file. USCIS believes it is reasonable to operate under the assumption that potential filers with legal representation would likely be advised not to file. USCIS evaluated the (c)(8) data and determined on average about half of the population uses a representative for their (c)(8) EAD request. Therefore, about half the population could incur filing cost-savings and about half would incur sunk costs associated with filing for an EAD that would be denied. In the net, the impact would be close to zero as costs would offset savings.[319]

Eligibility Bars

(8) CT2 = {POP × APV × (OYB + EWI−OCF) × HWG}
(8) CT2 = {POP × APV × (OYB + EWI−OCF) × HWG}

EAD时钟：申请及处理时间的变更
EAD Clock: Changes to Filing and Processing Time
适用于(c)(8) EAD申请与处理时间的变更共同影响收入时钟。根据拟议规则，EAD申请等待时间将从180天增加至365天，即申请人需要在拟议规则下等待更长时间才能提出申请（“EAD时钟”）。此外，拟议规则将使USCIS拥有更多时间进行裁决和处理，处理时间将从基准的30天延长至180天。衡量受影响收入的指标为EAD“等待时间”的差异，即庇护申请受理之日起至(c)(8) EAD批准之日止的时间间隔。等待时间为两部分之和，即EAD申请时间（FTC、FTF）与处理时间，其中“C”表示当前或过去，“F”表示未来或本规则下的情况。为估算FTF，我们采用一种简单的行为分析方法；对于过去等待时间少于365天的情况，FTF将设定为365天；例如，若某人过去在第200天提出申请，则在新规下其将在新最低值（365天）提出申请。对于过去等待时间超过365天的情况，则按其实际过去数值进行设定；例如，若某人过去等待了400天才提出申请，则未来其将在相同的时间点提出申请。该行为体系表明，等待时间少于365天者在新规下将无条件按新最低值提出申请，而其他人则不会受到申请时钟变更的影响。
The changes applicable to the (c)(8) EAD filing and processing time jointly affect the earnings clock. Under the proposed rule, the EAD filing time would increase from 180 days to 365 days, where an applicant would need to wait longer to file under the proposed rule (“EAD clock”). Further, the proposed rule would provide USCIS additional time to adjudicate and process from the baseline 30 days to 180 days. The metric to affected earnings will be the difference in the EAD “wait time,” which constitutes the duration between the asylum receipt claim and the approval of the (c)(8) EAD. The wait time is the sum of two components, the EAD filing time (FTC, FTF) and the process time, in which “C” denotes current, or past, and “F” is the future, or conditions under the rule. To estimate FTF we employ a simple behavioral approach; FTF will be set at 365 applicable to past values less than this value; if someone filed at 200 days in the past they would file at the new minimum (365). For those who filed past 365 days in the past, they would be assigned their actual past value; if someone waited 400 days to file, they would file at that same time in the future. This behavioral system imparts that those filing under 365 days would file unconditionally at the new minimum, whereas others would not be impacted by the filing clock change.
DHS希望获得足够的时间，对(c)(8) EAD申请进行审慎而全面的审查，包括针对国家安全和公共安全问题的筛查与审核。尽管DHS无法准确预测未来(c)(8) EAD的处理时间将会如何，但在本分析进行之时，我们认为大多数EAD可在约120天内完成裁决，且90天是一个合理的集中点。然而，DHS将允许最长180天的处理窗口，尽管个别案件可能因各种原因而需要更长时间。DHS强调，我们作为集中点所依据的90天处理时间（以及作为大多数(c)(8) EAD申请处理时间上限参考的120天）并非预测值，因为处理时间可能会因个案逐案分析或运营、资源、政策及移民状况的变化等原因而有所不同（原文第8685页）。相反，这些数值是使我们的估算程序具有可操作性所需的输入值，并基于DHS目前认为合理的判断。
DHS seeks sufficient time to conduct diligent and thorough review, to include screening and vetting for national security and public safety concerns, of (c)(8) EAD applications. While DHS cannot predict exactly what (c)(8) EAD process times will look like in the future, at the time of this analysis we believe that most EADs can be adjudicated within about 120 days and that 90 days is a reasonable cluster point. However, DHS will allow a window of up to 180 days, though an individual case could take longer for any number of reasons. DHS emphasizes that process times of 90 days that we rely on as a cluster point (and 120 days as an upper end range for most (c)(8) EADs applications) is not a prediction, as the process time could vary for reasons linked to case-by-case analysis of individual cases or changes in ( printed page 8685) operations, resources, policies, and immigration. Rather these values are inputs required to make our estimation procedures tractable and are based on what DHS thinks is currently reasonable.
就其对未来处理时间的适用而言,有必要采用一种数据转换方法,将每个过去的数值映射为未来的数值。从这个意义上说,由于未来情况将根据过去数据绘制得出,处理时间也具有行为性要素。但是,由于涉及转换,该设置属于准行为性质。数据必须被映射到一个满足多项政策与运营目标的新处理时间结构。首先,从数据分析中得出的最短处理时间(1天)是不可行的,有必要将该最小值提高,以便为国土安全部(DHS)在裁决过程中的审慎尽责留出空间。国土安全部的分析显示,(c)(8)类就业授权文件(EAD)当前处理时间的数据结构呈正偏态,均值大于中位数。由于潜在的数据聚集区间将接近90天,接近120天的上限区间,因此该数据结构需要重新居中至上段区域,呈负偏态并以120天为上限。需要指出的是,实际上限可能超过120天,但这是我们所采用模型的必要特征,我们将在下文中加以说明。此外,国土安全部还寻求裁决的一致性,因此降低方差也是一个目标。
As it applies to the future process time it is necessary to employ a data transformation mapping each past value to a future one. In this sense, because the future will be charted from past data, there is a behavioral element to the process time also. But, because a transformation is involved, the setup is quasi-behavioral. The data must be mapped to a new process time structure that satisfies multiple policy and operational goals. First, the minimum process time informed from the data analysis (1 day) is not tenable, and it is requisite that this minimum will rise to allow DHS diligence in the adjudicative process. DHS analysis reveals that the data structure for the (c)(8) EAD current process time is positively skewed, with a mean that is larger than the median. Since the potential cluster will be around 90 days, which is near the upper window of 120 days, the data structure will need to be recentered near the upper segment and negatively skewed and bounded to 120. It is noted that the actual upper bound may be more than 120 days, but it is a necessary feature of the model that we employ, which we will address downstream. Additionally, DHS seeks consistency in adjudications and therefore a lower variance is a goal.
方法论制定的这一部分具有技术性质,虽然此处提供了一些细节,但随附本规则制定的技术附录详细说明了估算程序的各个步骤。具体而言,国土安全部首先尝试运用多学科工作中常用的简单且常见的转换程序,对处理时间进行缩放以满足上限要求。虽然这些方法并非直接为将数据限定至上限而设计,但国土安全部能够调整算法以满足上限要求,然而由于其他原因,结果并不可行。由于这两种常见程序存在不足,国土安全部接下来采用了逻辑斯谛函数形式,该形式是众多自然科学与医学领域用于模拟增长模式的基准方法。该形式也是深度学习网络传播与训练中的一项关键开发实践。就深度学习网络而言,逻辑斯谛形式被用作一种数据转换工具,其设置可直接限定上限。然而,基于其通用且可扩展形式进行的试运行并未产生可行的结果。概括而言,经转换的处理时间趋于集中至某一特定数值,这是一个被称为“梯度消失”的技术性问题所导致的结果。
This part of the methodology development is technical in nature, and while some details are provided here, the technical appendix accompanying this rulemaking walks through the steps of the estimation procedures in detail. Specifically, DHS began by attempting to scale the process times to satisfy the upper bound, using simple and common transformation procedures utilized in multidisciplinary work. While these methods are not directly set up to bound data to an upper level, DHS was able to adjust the algorithms to satisfy the upper bound, but the results were not tenable for other reasons. Because of the inadequacies applicable the two common procedures, DHS next employed the logistic functional form, which is the benchmark to model growth patterns across a number of natural and medical sciences. It has also been a key development practice in the propagation and training of deep learning networks. As it relates to deep learning networks, the logistic form is utilized as a data transformation tool, and offers a setup that directly bounds an upper limit. However, trial runs based on its general and scalable form did not produce tenable results. In summary, the transformed process times gravitated to a specific value, which is a result of a technically involved issue known as a vanishing gradient.
鉴于上述结果不可行,国土安全部出于必要转而采用一种较新型的函数,该函数将逻辑斯谛形式纳入更为灵活但也更为复杂的结构中。该形式为第1型双曲线方程(hyperbolastic equation of Type 1),记为“H1”,其采用三个调节参数,并将反双曲正弦函数(ASINH)嵌套于逻辑斯谛方程的指数项中。这些调节参数通过试错法获得,而非基于规则的方法。就未来处理时间而言,H1中所采用的调节参数设定已实现既定目标:EAD最短处理时间增加至24天;数据在上尾部聚集(中位数为90天),中位数大于均值,表明分布已重新居中并向左偏斜;方差(以标准差衡量)已从41.5下降至28.3,降幅近三分之一(31.8%)。
Because of the untenable results noted above, out of necessity DHS turned to a newer type of function that incorporates a logistic form into a more flexible but also complex configuration. This form is a hyperbolastic equation of Type 1, denoted “H1” employs three tuning parameters and nests the inverse hyperbolic sine (ASINH) in the exponential term of the logistic equation. The tuners are obtained by trial and error, not by a rule-base method. For the future process times, the calibration of tuner settings employed in H1 has accomplished the stated objectives; the minimum EAD process time increased to 24 days; the data cluster in the upper tail (median=90) which is greater than the mean, indicating the distribution has been recentered and skewed leftward, and the variance (as measured by the standard deviation) has declined from 41.5 to 28.3, which is reduction of almost a third (31.8 percent).
针对每一个案件,国土安全部根据数据集计算出EAD的过去等待时间(WTP),并根据上述模型计算出未来等待时间(WTF),进而计算出等待时间差(WTD)。国土安全部将其(c)(8)类EAD的当前处理时间映射为新的处理时间。由此得出的分析表明,在本规则拟议条款下,几乎所有案件(98.1%)的等待时间都将延长,中位数为216天;而一小部分案件(1.9%)的等待时间可能缩短,从而获得收益时间,即WTP大于WTF(较短等待时间的范围为1天至98天,中位数为34天)。为将这些结果推广至整个群体,国土安全部将延误收益设为正值,将收益设为负值,以表示净效应,并根据WTD的分布与参数将其纳入计算。
For every case, DHS calculated the past wait time (WTP) for an EAD from the data set and a future wait (WTF) from the model developed above and then calculated the wait time difference (WTD). DHS mapped the current process time for their (c)(8) EAD to a new one. The resultant analysis suggests that almost all (98.1 percent) would experience a longer wait time under the proposed provisions of the rule, with a median of 216 days, and a small share (1.9 percent) could experience shortened wait time and gain earnings time, as WTP > WTF (the range for the shorter wait times is 1 day to 98 days, with a median of 34 days). To extend these results to the population, DHS set up the earnings delay as positive figures and the gains as negatives, to express net effects and incorporated the WTD according to its distribution and parameters.
Before turning to the simulation and results, we highlight an additional feature of the utilized data structure. It was necessary to stipulate an upper bound, which is 120 days in the current calibration ( i.e., when DHS believes most EADs can be adjudicated). But this window is not absolute, as DHS will allow a process time of up to 180 days with any case taking necessarily longer due to security or vetting concerns. The WTD distribution is not finite in its upper tail as we allow for longer wait times (driven by longer process times)— i.e. they are not ruled out in the seed trials. Hence, although DHS did not explicitly input longer process times in the estimation mechanism, we can ensconce this possibility without compromising any functionality of the system. In fact, DHS verifies and reports recursively that this effect has been rendered, as a small number of trials resulted in a WTD that exceeded the actual values in the sample (and 180 days)
For aliens that file for an EAD, which to reiterate, is the defensive population not subject to the proposed bars, the impact pertinent to filing for the EAD is the proposed change in the form burden (ΔFMB), which is 0.34 hours. Hence there will be a relatively small increased filing time burden to each individual that files, which DHS denotes as CST . Drawing on a common term for or the clock changes (“CT3”),
(11) CT3 = POP × APV (1−OYB−EWI + OCF) × HWG).
